I need to find a quoting bug in the desloppify triage runner. The bug manifests during the "organize" stage of `desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner codex`. The symptom is `zsh: unmatched '` when the runner generates a shell command to record permanent skips (something like `plan skip --permanent`).

Search thoroughly for:
1. The triage runner code that handles `--run-stages` and the organize stage
2. Any code that generates shell commands containing single quotes, especially around `plan skip --permanent`
3. How skip reasons or cluster names get interpolated into shell commands (likely the source of unescaped quotes)

Look in:
- desloppify/app/commands/ (especially anything related to plan, triage, scan)
- desloppify/engine/_plan/ (especially triage/)
- Any file mentioning "organize", "run-stages", "runner", "skip --permanent"

Be very thorough - read the full content of relevant files.

```
1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result
    10→from ..completion_flow import count_log_activity_since
    11→from ..review_coverage import open_review_ids_from_state
    12→from ..stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue
    13→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    14→from ..validation.stage_policy import (
    15→    ReflectAutoConfirmDeps,
    16→    auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.organize_policy import (
    19→    _clusters_enriched_or_error,
    20→    _manual_clusters_or_error,
    21→    _organize_report_or_error,
    22→    _unclustered_review_issues_or_error,
    23→    _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error,
    24→)
    25→from ..validation.core import _require_reflect_stage_for_organize
    26→from .records import record_organize_stage
    27→
    28→
    29→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict,
    32→    stages: dict,
    33→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    34→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    35→    is_reuse: bool,
    36→    attestation: str | None,
    37→) -> bool:
    38→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    39→    if not open_review_ids:
    40→        return True
    41→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    42→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    43→        return True
    44→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    45→    cluster_ops = sum(
    46→        activity.get(key, 0)
    47→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    48→    )
    49→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    50→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    51→        return True
    52→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    56→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→        return True
    61→    print(
    62→        colorize(
    63→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    64→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    65→            "red",
    66→        )
    67→    )
    68→    print(
    69→        colorize(
    70→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    71→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    72→            "dim",
    73→        )
    74→    )
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )
    81→    return False
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_organize_submission(
    85→    *,
    86→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    state: dict,
    89→    stages: dict,
    90→    report: str | None,
    91→    attestation: str | None,
    92→    is_reuse: bool,
    93→    services: TriageServices,
    94→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
    95→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    96→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    97→    if not auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    98→        args=args,
    99→        plan=plan,
   100→        stages=stages,
   101→        attestation=attestation,
   102→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   103→            triage_input=triage_input,
   104→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   105→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   106→        ),
   107→    ):
   108→        return None
   109→
   110→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   111→    if manual_clusters is None:
   112→        return None
   113→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   114→        return None
   115→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   116→        return None
   117→    if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   118→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   119→    ):
   120→        return None
   121→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   122→        plan=plan,
   123→        stages=stages,
   124→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   125→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   126→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→    ):
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   132→    if normalized_report is None:
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   136→        format_evidence_failures,
   137→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   141→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   142→        print(
   143→            colorize(
   144→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   145→                "red",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→        return None
   149→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   150→
   151→
   152→def _persist_organize_stage(
   153→    *,
   154→    plan: dict,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    report: str,
   157→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   158→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   159→    is_reuse: bool,
   160→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   161→    services: TriageServices,
   162→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   163→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   164→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   165→        stages,
   166→        report=report,
   167→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   168→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   169→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   170→    )
   171→    services.save_plan(plan)
   172→    services.append_log_entry(
   173→        plan,
   174→        "triage_organize",
   175→        actor="user",
   176→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   177→    )
   178→    services.save_plan(plan)
   179→    return cleared, stages
   180→
   181→
   182→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   183→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   184→    *,
   185→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   188→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   189→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   190→
   191→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   192→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   193→
   194→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   195→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   196→        return
   197→
   198→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   199→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   200→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   201→
   202→    is_reuse = False
   203→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   204→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   205→        is_reuse = True
   206→
   207→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   211→    state = runtime.state
   212→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   213→
   214→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   215→        args=args,
   216→        plan=plan,
   217→        state=state,
   218→        stages=stages,
   219→        report=report,
   220→        attestation=attestation,
   221→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   222→        services=resolved_services,
   223→    )
   224→    if validated is None:
   225→        return
   226→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   227→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   228→        plan=plan,
   229→        meta=meta,
   230→        report=normalized_report,
   231→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   232→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   233→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   234→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   235→        services=resolved_services,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    print_organize_result(
   239→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        report=normalized_report,
   242→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   243→        cleared=cleared,
   244→        stages=stages,
   245→    )
   246→
   247→
   248→def cmd_stage_organize(
   249→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   250→    *,
   251→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Public entrypoint for organize stage recording."""
   254→    _cmd_stage_organize(args, services=services)
   255→
   256→
   257→__all__ = [
   258→    "_cmd_stage_organize",
   259→    "cmd_stage_organize",
   260→]
   261→
```

```
   134→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   135→class StageExecutionDependencies:
   136→    """Dependency container for stage execution to support focused patching in tests."""
   137→
   138→    build_stage_prompt: Callable[..., str]
   139→    run_triage_stage: Callable[..., TriageStageRunResult]
   140→    read_stage_output: Callable[[Path], str]
   141→    analyze_reflect_issue_accounting: Callable[..., tuple[set[str], list[str], list[str]]]
   142→    validate_reflect_issue_accounting: Callable[
   143→        ...,
   144→        tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]],
   145→    ]
   146→
   147→
   148→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   149→class StageExecutionResult:
   150→    """Explicit outcome model for one stage-execution step."""
   151→
   152→    status: str
   153→    payload: dict[str, Any]
   154→    used_parallel: bool = False
   155→    output_file: Path | None = None
   156→    elapsed_seconds: int | None = None
   157→
   158→
   159→def default_stage_execution_dependencies() -> StageExecutionDependencies:
   160→    """Construct the default stage execution dependency set."""
   161→    return StageExecutionDependencies(
   162→        build_stage_prompt=build_stage_prompt,
   163→        run_triage_stage=run_triage_stage,
   164→        read_stage_output=read_stage_output,
   165→        analyze_reflect_issue_accounting=analyze_reflect_issue_accounting,
   166→        validate_reflect_issue_accounting=validate_reflect_accounting,
   167→    )
   168→
   169→
   170→def stage_report_recorded(plan: Mapping[str, Any], stage: str) -> bool:
   171→    """True when the plan contains a persisted report for the given stage."""
   172→    return bool(
   173→        plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   174→        .get("triage_stages", {})
   175→        .get(stage, {})
   176→        .get("report", "")
   177→    )
   178→
   179→
   180→def preflight_stage(
   181→    *,
   182→    stage: str,
   183→    prompt_mode: PromptMode = "output_only",
   184→    plan: Mapping[str, Any],
   185→    triage_input: Any,
   186→    dry_run: bool,
   187→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None],
   188→    validate_reflect_issue_accounting: Callable[
   189→        ...,
   190→        tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]],
   191→    ],
   192→) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
   193→    """Fail fast when a requested stage has invalid upstream prerequisites."""
   194→    # Dry-run previews do not persist upstream stage reports, so preflight checks that
   195→    # require recorded reflect/enrich state would otherwise fail by construction.
   196→    if dry_run and stage in {"organize", "sense-check"}:
   197→        return True, None
   198→
   199→    if stage == "sense-check":
   200→        stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   201→        progress = compute_triage_progress(stages)
   202→        if "sense-check" in stages or progress.current_stage == "sense-check":
   203→            return True, None
   204→        reason = "enrich_not_confirmed"
   205→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   206→        return False, reason
   207→
   208→    if stage != "organize":
   209→        return True, None
   210→    stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   211→    reflect_report = str(stages.get("reflect", {}).get("report", ""))
   212→    accounting_ok, _cited, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   213→        report=reflect_report,
   214→        valid_ids=set(getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}).keys()),
   215→    )
   216→    if not accounting_ok:
   217→        reason_parts: list[str] = []
   218→        if missing_ids:
   219→            reason_parts.append(f"missing={len(missing_ids)}")
   220→        if duplicate_ids:
   221→            reason_parts.append(f"duplicates={len(duplicate_ids)}")
   222→        reason = "reflect_accounting_invalid"
   223→        if reason_parts:
   224→            reason = f"{reason}({' '.join(reason_parts)})"
   225→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   226→        return False, reason
   227→
   228→    # Self-record stages mutate plan state from within the subagent, so their
   229→    # postconditions cannot be required before launch. The organize command
   230→    # itself validates ledger/disposition alignment when it records the stage.
   231→    if prompt_mode == "self_record":
   232→        return True, None
   233→
   234→    # Output-only organize paths may rely on pre-applied plan mutations.
   235→    ledger_mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   236→        plan=dict(plan), stages=stages,
   237→    )
   238→    if ledger_mismatches:
   239→        mismatch_types = set()
   240→        for m in ledger_mismatches:
   241→            mismatch_types.add(m.expected_decision)
   242→        reason = f"reflect_ledger_mismatch(count={len(ledger_mismatches)} types={','.join(sorted(mismatch_types))})"
   243→        append_run_log(f"stage-preflight-failed stage={stage} reason={reason}")
   244→        return False, reason
   245→
   246→    return True, None
   247→
   248→
   249→def build_reflect_repair_prompt(
   250→    *,
   251→    triage_input: Any,
   252→    prior_reports: Mapping[str, str],
   253→    repo_root: Path,
   254→    cli_command: str,
   255→    original_report: str,
   256→    missing_ids: list[str],
   257→    duplicate_ids: list[str],
   258→    build_stage_prompt_fn: Callable[..., str],
   259→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   260→) -> str:
   261→    """Build a targeted retry prompt for a reflect report that failed accounting."""
   262→    missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing_ids) or "none"
   263→    duplicate_short = (
   264→        ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicate_ids) or "none"
   265→    )
   266→    base_prompt = build_stage_prompt_fn(
   267→        "reflect",
   268→        triage_input,
   269→        dict(prior_reports),
   270→        repo_root=repo_root,
   271→        mode="output_only",
   272→        cli_command=cli_command,
   273→        stages_data=stages_data,
   274→    )
   275→    return "\n\n".join(
   276→        [
   277→            base_prompt,
   278→            "## Repair Pass",
   279→            "Your previous reflect report failed the exact-hash accounting check.",
   280→            f"Missing hashes: {missing_short}",
   281→            f"Duplicated hashes: {duplicate_short}",
   282→            "Rewrite the FULL reflect report so it passes validation.",
   283→            "Requirements for this repair:",
   284→            "- Start with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.",
   285→            '- Use one ledger line per issue hash: `- abcd1234 -> cluster "name"` or `- abcd1234 -> skip "reason"`.',
   286→            "- Mention every required hash exactly once in that ledger.",
   287→            "- Do not mention hashes anywhere else in the report.",
   288→            "- Preserve the same strategy unless fixing the missing/duplicate hashes forces a small adjustment.",
   289→            "- Output only the corrected reflect report.",
   290→            "## Previous Reflect Report",
   291→            original_report,
   292→        ]
   293→    )
   294→
   295→
   296→def repair_reflect_report_if_needed(
   297→    *,
   298→    report: str,
   299→    triage_input: Any,
   300→    prior_reports: Mapping[str, str],
   301→    repo_root: Path,
   302→    prompts_dir: Path,
   303→    output_dir: Path,
   304→    logs_dir: Path,
   305→    cli_command: str,
   306→    timeout_seconds: int,
   307→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None],
   308→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   309→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   310→) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
   311→    """Retry reflect once with a targeted repair prompt when accounting is invalid."""
   312→    _cited, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = dependencies.analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   313→        report=report,
   314→        valid_ids=set(getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}).keys()),
   315→    )
   316→    if not missing_ids and not duplicate_ids:
   317→        return report, None
   318→
   319→    print(colorize("  Reflect: repairing missing/duplicate hash accounting...", "yellow"))
   320→    append_run_log(
   321→        "stage-reflect-repair-start "
   322→        f"missing={len(missing_ids)} duplicates={len(duplicate_ids)}"
   323→    )
   324→
   325→    repair_prompt = build_reflect_repair_prompt(
   326→        triage_input=triage_input,
   327→        prior_reports=prior_reports,
   328→        repo_root=repo_root,
   329→        cli_command=cli_command,
   330→        original_report=report,
   331→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   332→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   333→        build_stage_prompt_fn=dependencies.build_stage_prompt,
   334→        stages_data=stages_data,
   335→    )
   336→    repair_prompt_file = prompts_dir / "reflect.repair.md"
   337→    repair_output_file = output_dir / "reflect.repair.raw.txt"
   338→    repair_log_file = logs_dir / "reflect.repair.log"
   339→    safe_write_text(repair_prompt_file, repair_prompt)
   340→    stage_result = dependencies.run_triage_stage(
   341→        prompt=repair_prompt,
   342→        repo_root=repo_root,
   343→        output_file=repair_output_file,
   344→        log_file=repair_log_file,
   345→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   346→    )
   347→    append_run_log(f"stage-reflect-repair-done code={stage_result.exit_code}")
   348→    if not stage_result.ok:
   349→        return None, f"reflect_repair_failed_exit_{stage_result.exit_code}"
   350→
   351→    repaired_report = dependencies.read_stage_output(repair_output_file)
   352→    if not repaired_report:
   353→        return None, "reflect_repair_empty_output"
   354→
   355→    _cited, missing_after, duplicates_after = dependencies.analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   356→        report=repaired_report,
   357→        valid_ids=set(getattr(triage_input, "open_issues", {}).keys()),
   358→    )
   359→    if missing_after or duplicates_after:
   360→        return None, "reflect_repair_invalid"
   361→
   362→    print(colorize("  Reflect: repair pass fixed issue accounting.", "green"))
   363→    append_run_log("stage-reflect-repair-success")
   364→    return repaired_report, None
   365→
   366→
   367→def _execute_parallel_stage(
   368→    *,
   369→    context: StageRunContext,
   370→    stage: str,
   371→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   372→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   373→    """Execute optional parallel stage path."""
   374→    if handler is None or handler.run_parallel is None:
   375→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   376→
   377→    parallel_result = handler.run_parallel(context)
   378→    if parallel_result.status == "dry_run":
   379→        return StageExecutionResult(
   380→            status="dry_run",
   381→            payload={"status": "dry_run"},
   382→            used_parallel=True,
   383→        )
   384→
   385→    if parallel_result.ok and parallel_result.merged_output:
   386→        if handler.record_report is not None:
   387→            handler.record_report(parallel_result.merged_output, context.args, context.services)
   388→            return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={}, used_parallel=True)
   389→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   390→
   391→    if parallel_result.ok:
   392→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   393→
   394→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   395→    error_reason = parallel_result.reason or "parallel_execution_failed"
   396→    print(colorize(f"  {stage.capitalize()}: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   397→    context.append_run_log(
   398→        f"stage-failed stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s reason={error_reason}"
   399→    )
   400→    return StageExecutionResult(
   401→        status="failed",
   402→        payload={
   403→            "status": "failed",
   404→            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   405→            "error": error_reason,
   406→        },
   407→        used_parallel=True,
   408→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   409→    )
   410→
   411→
   412→def _failure_result(
   413→    *,
   414→    stage: str,
   415→    elapsed: int | None,
   416→    error: str,
   417→    append_run_log: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
   418→    log_event: str = "stage-failed",
   419→    log_reason: str | None = None,
   420→    printed_message: str | None = None,
   421→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   422→    if printed_message is not None:
   423→        print(colorize(printed_message, "red"))
   424→    if append_run_log is not None:
   425→        log_parts = [f"{log_event} stage={stage}"]
   426→        if elapsed is not None:
   427→            log_parts.append(f"elapsed={elapsed}s")
   428→        log_parts.append(f"reason={log_reason or error}")
   429→        append_run_log(" ".join(log_parts))
   430→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   431→        "status": "failed",
   432→        "error": error,
   433→    }
   434→    if elapsed is not None:
   435→        payload["elapsed_seconds"] = elapsed
   436→    return StageExecutionResult(
   437→        status="failed",
   438→        payload=payload,
   439→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   440→    )
   441→
   442→
   443→def _build_subprocess_prompt(
   444→    *,
   445→    context: StageRunContext,
   446→    stage: str,
   447→    prompt_mode: PromptMode,
   448→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   449→) -> tuple[str, Mapping[str, Any]]:
   450→    """Build and persist one stage prompt for subprocess execution."""
   451→    stages_data = context.plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
   452→    prompt = dependencies.build_stage_prompt(
   453→        stage,
   454→        context.triage_input,
   455→        dict(context.prior_reports),
   456→        repo_root=context.repo_root,
   457→        mode=prompt_mode,
   458→        cli_command=context.cli_command,
   459→        stages_data=stages_data,
   460→    )
   461→    prompt_file = context.prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   462→    safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   463→    return prompt, stages_data
   464→
   465→
   466→def _run_subprocess_stage(
   467→    *,
   468→    context: StageRunContext,
   469→    stage: str,
   470→    prompt: str,
   471→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   472→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   473→    """Run codex subprocess for one stage (or emit dry-run status)."""
   474→    prompt_file = context.prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   475→    if context.dry_run:
   476→        print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: prompt written to {prompt_file}", "cyan"))
   477→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute codex subprocess.", "dim"))
   478→        return StageExecutionResult(
   479→            status="dry_run",
   480→            payload={"status": "dry_run"},
   481→        )
   482→
   483→    print(colorize(f"\n  Stage {stage}: launching codex subprocess...", "bold"))
   484→    context.append_run_log(f"stage-subprocess-start stage={stage}")
   485→
   486→    output_file = context.output_dir / f"{stage}.raw.txt"
   487→    log_file = context.logs_dir / f"{stage}.log"
   488→    stage_result = dependencies.run_triage_stage(
   489→        prompt=prompt,
   490→        repo_root=context.repo_root,
   491→        output_file=output_file,
   492→        log_file=log_file,
   493→        timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   494→    )
   495→
   496→    elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   497→    context.append_run_log(
   498→        "stage-subprocess-done "
   499→        f"stage={stage} code={stage_result.exit_code} elapsed={elapsed}s"
   500→    )
   501→
   502→    if stage_result.ok:
   503→        return StageExecutionResult(
   504→            status="ready",
   505→            payload={},
   506→            output_file=output_file,
   507→            elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   508→        )
   509→
   510→    print(
   511→        colorize(
   512→            "  Stage "
   513→            f"{stage}: codex subprocess failed (exit {stage_result.exit_code}).",
   514→            "red",
   515→        )
   516→    )
   517→    print(colorize(f"  Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   518→    print(colorize("  Re-run to resume (confirmed stages are skipped).", "dim"))
   519→    context.append_run_log(
   520→        "stage-failed "
   521→        f"stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s code={stage_result.exit_code}"
   522→    )
   523→    return StageExecutionResult(
   524→        status="failed",
   525→        payload={
   526→            "status": "failed",
   527→            "exit_code": stage_result.exit_code,
   528→            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
   529→        },
   530→        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
   531→    )
   532→
   533→
   534→def _resolve_subprocess_artifacts(
   535→    subprocess_result: StageExecutionResult,
   536→) -> tuple[Path, int] | None:
   537→    output_file = subprocess_result.output_file
   538→    elapsed = subprocess_result.elapsed_seconds
   539→    if output_file is None or elapsed is None:
   540→        return None
   541→    return output_file, elapsed
   542→
   543→
   544→def _record_stage_report_if_needed(
   545→    *,
   546→    context: StageRunContext,
   547→    stage: str,
   548→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   549→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   550→    output_file: Path,
   551→    elapsed: int,
   552→    stages_data: Mapping[str, Any],
   553→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   554→    """Record subprocess output for stages that require orchestrator persistence."""
   555→    if handler is None or handler.record_report is None:
   556→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   557→
   558→    report = dependencies.read_stage_output(output_file)
   559→    if not report:
   560→        return _failure_result(
   561→            stage=stage,
   562→            elapsed=elapsed,
   563→            error="empty_stage_output",
   564→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   565→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: output file was empty after subprocess.",
   566→        )
   567→
   568→    if stage == "reflect":
   569→        report, repair_error = repair_reflect_report_if_needed(
   570→            report=report,
   571→            triage_input=context.triage_input,
   572→            prior_reports=context.prior_reports,
   573→            repo_root=context.repo_root,
   574→            prompts_dir=context.prompts_dir,
   575→            output_dir=context.output_dir,
   576→            logs_dir=context.logs_dir,
   577→            cli_command=context.cli_command,
   578→            timeout_seconds=context.timeout_seconds,
   579→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   580→            dependencies=dependencies,
   581→            stages_data=stages_data,
   582→        )
   583→        if repair_error:
   584→            return _failure_result(
   585→                stage=stage,
   586→                elapsed=elapsed,
   587→                error=repair_error,
   588→                append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   589→                printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: repair failed ({repair_error}).",
   590→            )
   591→        if not report:
   592→            return _failure_result(
   593→                stage=stage,
   594→                elapsed=elapsed,
   595→                error="reflect_repair_no_report",
   596→                append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   597→            )
   598→
   599→    handler.record_report(report, context.args, context.services)
   600→    plan_after_record = context.services.load_plan()
   601→    if not stage_report_recorded(plan_after_record, stage):
   602→        return _failure_result(
   603→            stage=stage,
   604→            elapsed=elapsed,
   605→            error="stage_not_recorded",
   606→            append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   607→            log_event="stage-record-failed",
   608→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: handler completed but did not persist the stage.",
   609→        )
   610→    context.append_run_log(
   611→        f"stage-recorded stage={stage} elapsed={elapsed}s mode=orchestrator"
   612→    )
   613→    return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   614→
   615→
   616→def _run_stage_subprocess_path(
   617→    *,
   618→    context: StageRunContext,
   619→    stage: str,
   620→    handler: StageHandler | None,
   621→    prompt_mode: PromptMode,
   622→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   623→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   624→    prompt, stages_data = _build_subprocess_prompt(
   625→        context=context,
   626→        stage=stage,
   627→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   628→        dependencies=dependencies,
   629→    )
   630→    subprocess_result = _run_subprocess_stage(
   631→        context=context,
   632→        stage=stage,
   633→        prompt=prompt,
   634→        dependencies=dependencies,
   635→    )
   636→    if subprocess_result.status != "ready":
   637→        return subprocess_result
   638→
   639→    subprocess_artifacts = _resolve_subprocess_artifacts(subprocess_result)
   640→    if subprocess_artifacts is None:
   641→        return _failure_result(
   642→            stage=stage,
   643→            elapsed=None,
   644→            error="subprocess_output_missing",
   645→        )
   646→    output_file, elapsed = subprocess_artifacts
   647→
   648→    return _record_stage_report_if_needed(
   649→        context=context,
   650→        stage=stage,
   651→        handler=handler,
   652→        dependencies=dependencies,
   653→        output_file=output_file,
   654→        elapsed=elapsed,
   655→        stages_data=stages_data,
   656→    )
   657→
   658→
   659→def execute_stage(
   660→    context: StageRunContext,
   661→    *,
   662→    handlers: Mapping[str, StageHandler],
   663→    dependencies: StageExecutionDependencies,
   664→) -> StageExecutionResult:
   665→    """Execute one stage and return an explicit stage outcome."""
   666→    stage = context.stage
   667→    handler = handlers.get(stage)
   668→    prompt_mode = handler.prompt_mode if handler is not None else "output_only"
   669→
   670→    preflight_ok, preflight_reason = preflight_stage(
   671→        stage=stage,
   672→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   673→        plan=context.plan,
   674→        triage_input=context.triage_input,
   675→        dry_run=context.dry_run,
   676→        append_run_log=context.append_run_log,
   677→        validate_reflect_issue_accounting=dependencies.validate_reflect_issue_accounting,
   678→    )
   679→    if not preflight_ok:
   680→        elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - context.stage_start)
   681→        return _failure_result(
   682→            stage=stage,
   683→            elapsed=elapsed,
   684→            error=preflight_reason or "stage_preflight_failed",
   685→            printed_message=f"  Stage {stage}: blocked before launch ({preflight_reason}).",
   686→        )
   687→
   688→    parallel_result = _execute_parallel_stage(
   689→        context=context,
   690→        stage=stage,
   691→        handler=handler,
   692→    )
   693→    if parallel_result.status != "ready":
   694→        return parallel_result
   695→    if parallel_result.used_parallel:
   696→        return StageExecutionResult(status="ready", payload={})
   697→    return _run_stage_subprocess_path(
   698→        context=context,
   699→        stage=stage,
   700→        handler=handler,
   701→        prompt_mode=prompt_mode,
   702→        dependencies=dependencies,
   703→    )
   704→
   705→
   706→__all__ = [
   707→    "DEFAULT_STAGE_HANDLERS",
   708→    "StageExecutionResult",
   709→    "StageExecutionDependencies",
   710→    "StageHandler",
   711→    "build_reflect_repair_prompt",
   712→    "default_stage_execution_dependencies",
   713→    "execute_stage",
   714→    "preflight_stage",
   715→    "read_stage_output",
   716→    "repair_reflect_report_if_needed",
   717→    "stage_report_recorded",
   718→]
   719→
```

```
   138→def test_validate_reflect_issue_accounting_prefers_coverage_ledger() -> None:
   139→    valid_ids = {
   140→        "review::src/a.ts::alpha::aaaabbbb",
   141→        "review::src/b.ts::beta::ccccdddd",
   142→    }
   143→    report = """
   144→## Coverage Ledger
   145→- aaaabbbb -> cluster "alpha"
   146→- ccccdddd -> skip "false-positive"
   147→
   148→## Cluster Blueprint
   149→Cluster "alpha" handles aaaabbbb after the shared seam is stable.
   150→"""
   151→    ok, cited, missing, duplicates = _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   152→        report=report,
   153→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   154→    )
   155→    assert ok
   156→    assert cited == valid_ids
   157→    assert missing == []
   158→    assert duplicates == []
   159→
   160→
   161→def test_validate_reflect_issue_accounting_accepts_named_short_ids() -> None:
   162→    valid_ids = {
   163→        "review::src/a.py::error_consistency::plugin_failures_look_like_absent_capabilities",
   164→        "review::src/b.py::error_consistency::policy_load_masks_corruption",
   165→    }
   166→    report = """
   167→## Coverage Ledger
   168→- plugin_failures_look_like_absent_capabilities -> cluster "plugin-load-error-semantics"
   169→- policy_load_masks_corruption -> skip "defer-to-followup"
   170→
   171→## Cluster Blueprint
   172→Cluster "plugin-load-error-semantics" owns loader error semantics.
   173→"""
   174→    ok, cited, missing, duplicates = _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   175→        report=report,
   176→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   177→    )
   178→    assert ok
   179→    assert cited == valid_ids
   180→    assert missing == []
   181→    assert duplicates == []
   182→
   183→
   184→def test_validate_reflect_issue_accounting_handles_short_id_collisions() -> None:
   185→    valid_ids = {
   186→        "review::src/a.py::cross_module_architecture::review_packet_ownership_split",
   187→        "review::src/b.py::high_level_elegance::review_packet_ownership_split",
   188→    }
   189→    report = """
   190→## Coverage Ledger
   191→- review_packet_ownership_split -> cluster "review-packet-lifecycle-ownership"
   192→- review_packet_ownership_split -> cluster "review-packet-lifecycle-ownership"
   193→
   194→## Cluster Blueprint
   195→Cluster "review-packet-lifecycle-ownership" owns packet lifecycle policy.
   196→"""
   197→    ok, cited, missing, duplicates = _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   198→        report=report,
   199→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   200→    )
   201→    assert ok
   202→    assert cited == valid_ids
   203→    assert missing == []
   204→    assert duplicates == []

   260→def _plan_with_stages(**kwargs: dict) -> dict:
   261→    """Create a plan with triage stages."""
   262→    return {
   263→        "epic_triage_meta": {
   264→            "triage_stages": kwargs,
   265→        },
   266→        "clusters": {},
   267→        "queue_order": [],
   268→    }
   269→
   270→
   271→def test_validate_observe_missing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
   272→    plan = _plan_with_stages()
   273→    ok, msg = validate_stage("observe", plan, {}, tmp_path)
   274→    assert not ok
   275→    assert "not recorded" in msg
   276→

   282→    assert "too short" in msg

   394→# ---------- Underspecified steps (AND→OR fix) ----------

   397→def test_underspecified_catches_refs_but_no_detail(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
   398→    """A step with issue_refs but no detail should be caught."""
   399→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage.validation.core import (
   400→        _underspecified_steps,
   401→    )
   402→
   403→    plan = {
   404→        "clusters": {
   405→            "c1": {
   406→                "issue_ids": ["review::a::b"],
   407→                "action_steps": [
   408→                    {"title": "shell step", "issue_refs": ["review::a::b"], "effort": "small"}
   409→                ],
   410→            }
   411→        }
   412→    }
   413→    results = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   414→    assert len(results) == 1
   415→    assert results[0][0] == "c1"
   416→    assert results[0][1] == 1  # 1 bare step
   417→

   549→def test_validate_completion_self_dependency(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

   555→        **{"sense-check": {"report": "x" * 150, "confirmed_at": "t"}},

   559→            "issue_ids": ["review::a::b"],

   561→            "action_steps": [{"title": "fix", "detail": "d", "issue_refs": ["review::a::b"]}],
   562→            "depends_on_clusters": ["self-dep"],

   565→    ok, msg = validate_completion(plan, {"issues": {"review::a::b": {"status": "open", "detector": "review"}}}, tmp_path)

   744→def test_sense_check_stage_in_pipeline_order() -> None:
   745→    """sense-check must appear between enrich and commit in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS."""
   746→    from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
   747→
   748→    ids = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   749→    assert "triage::sense-check" in ids
   750→    enrich_idx = ids.index("triage::enrich")
   751→    sense_idx = ids.index("triage::sense-check")
   752→    commit_idx = ids.index("triage::commit")
   753→    assert enrich_idx < sense_idx < commit_idx

   755→
   756→# ---------- Plan lock ----------
   757→
   758→
   759→def test_plan_lock_prevents_concurrent_writes(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
   760→    """plan_lock should serialize access via file locking."""
   761→    import threading
   762→
   763→    from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import plan_lock
   764→
   765→    plan_file = tmp_path / "plan.json"
   766→    plan_file.write_text("{}")
   767→
   768→    results: list[int] = []
   769→    barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
   770→
   771→    def worker(value: int) -> None:
   772→        barrier.wait()  # ensure both threads start ~simultaneously
   773→        with plan_lock(plan_file):
   774→            # If locking works, only one thread is in here at a time
   775→            current = len(results)
   776→            results.append(value)
   777→            assert len(results) == current + 1  # no interleaving
   778→
   779→    t1 = threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(1,))
   780→    t2 = threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(2,))
   781→    t1.start()
   782→    t2.start()
   783→    t1.join(timeout=5)
   784→    t2.join(timeout=5)
   785→
   786→    assert sorted(results) == [1, 2]
   787→
   788→

   793→    """Triage runner should validate plain text output by default (not JSON-only)."""
   794→    captured: dict[str, object] = {}

   796→    def _fake_run_codex_batch(*, prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file, deps, codex_batch_command_fn=None):
   797→        captured["prompt"] = prompt
   798→        captured["repo_root"] = repo_root
   799→        captured["output_file"] = output_file
   800→        captured["log_file"] = log_file
   801→        captured["validator"] = deps.validate_output_fn
   802→        return 0
   803→
   804→    monkeypatch.setattr(codex_runner, "run_codex_batch", _fake_run_codex_batch)
   805→
   806→    output_file = tmp_path / "triage.raw.txt"
   807→    log_file = tmp_path / "triage.log"
   808→    ret = codex_runner.run_triage_stage(

   814→    assert ret.ok
```

    11→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    12→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
    13→- Describe code that was already refactored
    14→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
    15→- Count props/returns/args wrong

    17→  to eliminate 5 lines of duplication)
    18→- Flag issues where the current code is "good enough" — imperfect but clear, simple,
    19→  and not causing real problems
    20→
    21→Your job is to catch these. A report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
    22→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
    23→
    24→Use the `not-worth-it` verdict when: the issue is technically real but the fix would add
    25→more complexity than it removes, or the current code is simple and readable despite being
    26→theoretically suboptimal. YAGNI — if nobody is actually confused or blocked by this code,
    27→it doesn't need fixing.

   164→```
   165→**Important:** The --attest value MUST contain the words "reviewed" and "not gaming" (exact phrases required by validation).

```
1→"""Organize-stage validation helpers and reflect-ledger reconciliation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

    10→from ..review_coverage import cluster_issue_ids, manual_clusters_with_issues
    11→from ..stages.helpers import unclustered_review_issues, unenriched_clusters
    12→from .reflect_accounting import ReflectDisposition
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class ActualDisposition:
    17→    """What actually happened to an issue in plan state."""
    18→
    19→    kind: Literal["clustered", "skipped", "unplaced"]
    20→    cluster_name: str = ""
    21→
    22→    def describe(self, intended: ReflectDisposition | None = None) -> str:
    23→        """Human-readable description for error messages."""
    24→        if self.kind == "skipped":
    25→            return "permanently skipped"
    26→        if self.kind == "clustered":
    27→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster" and self.cluster_name != intended.target:
    28→                return f'in cluster "{self.cluster_name}" (expected "{intended.target}")'
    29→            return f'clustered in "{self.cluster_name}"'
    30→        if self.kind == "unplaced":
    31→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster":
    32→                return "not in any cluster"
    33→            return "not skipped, not clustered"
    34→        return "unknown state"
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    38→class LedgerMismatch:
    39→    """One issue where plan state diverges from the reflect disposition."""
    40→
    41→    intended: ReflectDisposition
    42→    actual: ActualDisposition
    43→
    44→    @property
    45→    def issue_id(self) -> str:
    46→        return self.intended.issue_id
    47→
    48→    @property
    49→    def expected_decision(self) -> str:
    50→        return self.intended.decision
    51→
    52→    @property
    53→    def expected_target(self) -> str:
    54→        return self.intended.target
    55→
    56→    @property
    57→    def actual_state(self) -> str:
    58→        return self.actual.describe(self.intended)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _manual_clusters_or_error(
    62→    plan: dict,
    63→    *,
    64→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    65→) -> list[str] | None:
    66→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    67→    if manual_clusters:
    68→        return manual_clusters
    69→    if open_review_ids is not None and not open_review_ids:
    70→        return []
    71→    any_clusters = [
    72→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
    73→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    74→    ]
    75→    if any_clusters:
    76→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
    77→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
    78→    else:
    79→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
    80→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))

    83→
    84→
    85→def _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan: dict) -> bool:
    86→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    87→    if not gaps:
    88→        return True
    89→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
    90→    for name, missing in gaps:
    91→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    92→    print()
    93→    print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
    94→    print(
    95→        colorize(
    96→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" --steps "step 1" "step 2"',
    97→            "dim",
    98→        )
    99→    )
   100→    return False
   101→
   102→
   103→def _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   104→    """Block if open review issues aren't in any manual cluster. Return True if OK."""
   105→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   106→    if not unclustered:
   107→        return True
   108→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no cluster.", "red"))
   109→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   110→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   111→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   112→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
   113→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   114→    print()
   115→    print(colorize("  Every review issue needs an action plan. Either:", "dim"))
   116→    print(colorize("    1. Add to a cluster: desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern>", "dim"))
   117→    print(
   118→        colorize(
   119→            '    2. Wontfix it: desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "reason" --attest "..."',
   120→            "dim",
   121→        )
   122→    )
   123→    return False
   124→
   125→
   126→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   127→    if not report:
   128→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   131→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   132→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   133→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   134→        return None
   135→    if len(report) < 100:
   136→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   137→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   138→        return None
   139→    return report
   140→
   141→
   142→def _build_actual_disposition_index(plan: dict) -> dict[str, ActualDisposition]:
   143→    """Build a lookup from issue ID to its actual disposition in plan state."""
   144→    index: dict[str, ActualDisposition] = {}
   145→
   146→    for cluster_name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   147→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   148→            continue
   149→        for fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   150→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="clustered", cluster_name=cluster_name)
   151→
   152→    for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}):
   153→        if isinstance(fid, str):
   154→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="skipped")
   155→
   156→    return index
   157→
   158→
   159→def _disposition_matches(intended: ReflectDisposition, actual: ActualDisposition) -> bool:
   160→    """True when the actual plan state satisfies the intended disposition."""
   161→    if intended.decision == "permanent_skip":
   162→        return actual.kind == "skipped"
   163→    if intended.decision == "cluster":
   164→        return actual.kind == "clustered" and actual.cluster_name == intended.target
   165→    return False
   166→
   167→
   168→def validate_organize_against_dispositions(
   169→    *,
   170→    plan: dict,
   171→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   172→    """Check that plan mutations match the unified issue_dispositions map.
   173→
   174→    Preferred over ``validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger`` when the
   175→    disposition map exists.  Only checks entries with a ``decision`` field
   176→    (i.e. entries that have been through observe auto-skip or reflect).
   177→    """
   178→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   179→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions", {})
   180→    if not dispositions:
   181→        return []
   182→
   183→    # Only validate entries that have a decision
   184→    decided = [
   185→        (issue_id, disp)
   186→        for issue_id, disp in dispositions.items()
   187→        if disp.get("decision")
   188→    ]
   189→    if not decided:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   193→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   194→    mismatches: list[LedgerMismatch] = []
   195→
   196→    for issue_id, disp in decided:
   197→        decision = disp["decision"]
   198→        target = disp.get("target", "")
   199→        # Normalize to ReflectDisposition for the mismatch report
   200→        reflect_decision = "permanent_skip" if decision == "skip" else decision
   201→        intended = ReflectDisposition(
   202→            issue_id=issue_id,
   203→            decision=reflect_decision,
   204→            target=target,
   205→        )
   206→        actual = actuals.get(issue_id, unplaced)
   207→        if not _disposition_matches(intended, actual):
   208→            mismatches.append(LedgerMismatch(intended=intended, actual=actual))
   209→
   210→    return mismatches
   211→
   212→
   213→def validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   214→    *,
   215→    plan: dict,
   216→    stages: dict,
   217→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   218→    """Check that plan mutations match the reflect disposition ledger."""
   219→    raw_ledger = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("disposition_ledger")
   220→    if not raw_ledger:
   221→        return []
   222→
   223→    ledger = [ReflectDisposition.from_dict(d) for d in raw_ledger]
   224→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   225→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   226→
   227→    return [
   228→        LedgerMismatch(intended=d, actual=actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   229→        for d in ledger
   230→        if not _disposition_matches(d, actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   231→    ]
   232→
   233→
   234→def _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   235→    *,
   236→    plan: dict,
   237→    stages: dict,
   238→) -> bool:
   239→    """Block organize if plan state diverges from dispositions or the reflect ledger."""
   240→    # Prefer unified disposition map when available
   241→    mismatches = validate_organize_against_dispositions(plan=plan)
   242→    if not mismatches:
   243→        # Fall back to legacy reflect ledger validation
   244→        mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   245→            plan=plan,
   246→            stages=stages,
   247→        )
   248→    if not mismatches:
   249→        return True
   250→
   251→    print(
   252→        colorize(
   253→            f"  Cannot organize: {len(mismatches)} issue(s) diverge from the reflect plan.",
   254→            "red",
   255→        )
   256→    )
   257→    for mismatch in mismatches[:10]:
   258→        short_id = mismatch.issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   259→        print(
   260→            colorize(
   261→                f"    {short_id}: reflect said {mismatch.expected_decision} "
   262→                f'"{mismatch.expected_target}", but {mismatch.actual_state}',
   263→                "yellow",
   264→            )
   265→        )
   266→    if len(mismatches) > 10:
   267→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(mismatches) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   268→    print()
   269→    print(
   270→        colorize(
   271→            "  Fix the plan to match the reflect ledger, or re-run reflect to update dispositions.",
   272→            "dim",
   273→        )
   274→    )
   275→    return False
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = [
   279→    "ActualDisposition",
   280→    "LedgerMismatch",
   281→    "_clusters_enriched_or_error",
   282→    "_manual_clusters_or_error",
```

```
    34→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Return sorted short hashes for open review issues."""
    36→    return sorted(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in triage_input.open_issues)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    40→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    41→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    42→    for issue in triage_input.open_issues.values():
    43→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    44→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    45→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    46→    parts = [
    47→        "## Issue Summary",
    48→        f"Open review issues: {len(triage_input.open_issues)}",
    49→    ]
    50→    if dims:
    51→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    52→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    53→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    54→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    55→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    56→
    57→    # Cluster summary if clusters exist
    58→    existing = triage_input.existing_clusters or {}
    59→    if existing:
    60→        parts.append("")
    61→        parts.append("## Clusters")
    62→        for cname, cdata in existing.items():
    63→            desc = cdata.get("description") or ""
    64→            count = len(cdata.get("issue_ids", []))
    65→            desc_str = f" ({desc})" if desc else ""
    66→            parts.append(f"- {cname}: {count} issues{desc_str}")
    67→
    68→    return "\n".join(parts)
    69→

    71→_INVESTIGATION_COMMANDS = """\
    72→
    73→## Available Investigation Commands
    74→
    75→To see full detail for a specific issue:
    76→  desloppify show <issue-id-or-hash-pattern> --no-budget
    77→To see all open review issues with suggestions:
    78→  desloppify show review --status open --no-budget
    79→To see a cluster's steps, members, and suggestions:
    80→  desloppify plan cluster show <name>
    81→To list all clusters:
    82→  desloppify plan cluster list --verbose"""

    85→def _issue_context_for_stage(

    87→    triage_input: TriageInput,

    91→    if stage in {"observe", "reflect"}:
    92→        parts = ["## Issue Data\n\n" + build_triage_prompt(triage_input)]
    93→        if stage == "reflect":
    94→            short_ids = _required_issue_hashes(triage_input)
    95→            parts.append(
    96→                "## Required Issue Hashes\n"
    97→                f"Total open review issues: {len(short_ids)}\n"
    98→                "Every one of these hashes must appear exactly once in your cluster/skip blueprint.\n"
    99→                "Do not repeat hashes outside that blueprint.\n"
   100→                + ", ".join(short_ids)
   101→            )
   102→            parts.append(
   103→                "## Coverage Ledger Template\n"
   104→                "Your final report MUST contain a `## Coverage Ledger` section with one line per issue.\n"
   105→                "Allowed forms:\n"
   106→                '- `- abcd1234 -> cluster "cluster-name"`\n'
   107→                '- `- abcd1234 -> skip "specific-reason-tag"`\n'
   108→                "Do not mention hashes outside the `## Coverage Ledger` section.\n"
   109→                + "\n".join(f"- {short_id} -> TODO" for short_id in short_ids)
   110→            )
   111→        return "\n\n".join(parts)
   112→    summary = _compact_issue_summary(triage_input)
   113→    if mode == "self_record":

   117→
   118→def _format_assessments_table(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   119→    """Format structured observe assessments as a readable table for downstream stages."""
   120→    if not assessments:
   121→        return ""
   122→    lines = ["### Structured Observe Assessments\n"]
   123→    lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   124→    lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   125→    for a in assessments:
   126→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
   127→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
   128→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
   129→        # Truncate recommendation for table readability
   130→        if len(r) > 80:
   131→            r = r[:77] + "..."
   132→        lines.append(f"| {h} | {v} | {r} |")
   133→    return "\n".join(lines)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _format_disposition_table(
   137→    dispositions: dict[str, dict],
   138→    assessments: list[dict],
   139→) -> str:
   140→    """Format the unified disposition map for downstream stages.
   141→
   142→    Partitions issues into:
   143→    - Auto-skipped by observe (false-positive/exaggerated)
   144→    - Genuine issues requiring disposition
   145→    - Actionability-flagged issues (over-engineering/not-worth-it)
   146→    """
   147→    if not dispositions and not assessments:
   148→        return ""
   149→
   150→    auto_skipped: list[dict] = []
   151→    genuine: list[dict] = []
   152→    actionability: list[dict] = []
   153→
   154→    # Build from assessments for hash display, enriched by dispositions
   155→    for a in assessments:
   156→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
   157→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
   158→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
   159→        if len(r) > 80:
   160→            r = r[:77] + "..."
   161→        entry = {"hash": h, "verdict": v, "recommendation": r}
   162→
   163→        if v in ("false positive", "exaggerated"):
   164→            auto_skipped.append(entry)
   165→        elif v in ("over engineering", "not worth it"):
   166→            actionability.append(entry)
   167→        else:
   168→            genuine.append(entry)
   169→
   170→    lines = ["### Issue Disposition Summary\n"]
   171→
   172→    if auto_skipped:
   173→        lines.append(f"**Auto-skipped by observe ({len(auto_skipped)} issues)** — no action needed:\n")
   174→        lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   175→        lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   176→        for e in auto_skipped:
   177→            lines.append(f"| {e['hash']} | {e['verdict']} | {e['recommendation']} |")
   178→        lines.append("")
   179→
   180→    if genuine:
   181→        lines.append(f"**Genuine issues requiring disposition ({len(genuine)} issues):**\n")
   182→        lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   183→        lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   184→        for e in genuine:
   185→            lines.append(f"| {e['hash']} | {e['verdict']} | {e['recommendation']} |")
   186→        lines.append("")
   187→
   188→    if actionability:
   189→        lines.append(
   190→            f"**Actionability-flagged issues ({len(actionability)} issues)** "
   191→            "— observe thinks these aren't worth it, reflect decides:\n"
   192→        )
   193→        lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   194→        lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   195→        for e in actionability:
   196→            lines.append(f"| {e['hash']} | {e['verdict']} | {e['recommendation']} |")
   197→        lines.append("")
   198→
   199→    return "\n".join(lines)
   200→
   201→
   202→def _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   203→    """Format file-level observe evidence for clustering-heavy downstream stages."""
   204→    if not assessments:
   205→        return ""
   206→    lines = ["### Observe File Evidence\n"]
   207→    for assessment in assessments:
   208→        issue_hash = str(assessment.get("hash", "?"))
   209→        files_read = [
   210→            str(path).strip()
   211→            for path in assessment.get("files_read", [])
   212→            if str(path).strip()
   213→        ]
   214→        verdict = str(assessment.get("verdict", "?")).strip() or "?"
   215→        reasoning = str(assessment.get("verdict_reasoning", "")).strip()
   216→        recommendation = str(assessment.get("recommendation", "")).strip()
   217→        lines.append(f"- {issue_hash}: {verdict}")
   218→        if files_read:
   219→            lines.append(f"  files_read: {', '.join(files_read)}")
   220→        if reasoning:
   221→            lines.append(f"  verdict_reasoning: {reasoning}")
   222→        if recommendation:
   223→            lines.append(f"  recommendation: {recommendation}")
   224→    return "\n".join(lines)
   225→
   226→
   227→def _relevant_prior_reports(
   228→    stage: str,
   229→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   230→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   231→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   232→    """Return the stage reports that matter for the current stage."""
   233→    wanted = {
   234→        "reflect": ("observe",),
   235→        "organize": ("reflect",),
   236→        "enrich": ("organize",),
   237→        "sense-check": ("organize", "enrich"),
   238→    }.get(stage, tuple(prior_reports))
   239→    result = [(name, prior_reports[name]) for name in wanted if name in prior_reports]
   240→
   241→    # Append structured observe assessments / disposition table for downstream stages
   242→    if stage in {"reflect", "organize", "sense-check"} and stages_data:
   243→        observe_data = stages_data.get("observe", {})
   244→        assessments = observe_data.get("assessments", [])
   245→
   246→        # Use disposition table when dispositions exist, fall back to assessments table.
   247→        # Note: stages_data is the triage_stages dict; dispositions are on
   248→        # epic_triage_meta which we don't have here. Use assessments to build
   249→        # the partitioned view since the verdict info is the same.
   250→        if assessments:
   251→            disp_table = _format_disposition_table({}, assessments)
   252→            if disp_table:
   253→                result.append(("observe-dispositions", disp_table))
   254→            else:
   255→                table = _format_assessments_table(assessments)
   256→                if table:
   257→                    result.append(("observe-assessments", table))
   258→        if stage == "organize":
   259→            evidence = _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments)
   260→            if evidence:
   261→                result.append(("observe-evidence", evidence))
   262→
   263→    return result
   264→
   265→
   266→def build_stage_prompt(
   267→    stage: str,
   268→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   269→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   270→    *,
   271→    repo_root: Path,
   272→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
   273→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   274→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   275→) -> str:
   276→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   277→    parts: list[str] = []
   278→
   279→    # Preamble
   280→    parts.append(
   281→        triage_prompt_preamble(mode).format(
   282→            stage=stage.upper(),
   283→            repo_root=repo_root,
   284→            cli_command=cli_command,
   285→        )
   286→    )
   287→
   288→    # Prior stage reports
   289→    relevant_prior_reports = _relevant_prior_reports(stage, prior_reports, stages_data)
   290→    if relevant_prior_reports:
   291→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   292→        for prior_stage, report in relevant_prior_reports:
   293→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   294→
   295→    # Issue data / summary
   296→    parts.append(_issue_context_for_stage(stage, triage_input, mode))
   297→
   298→    # Stage-specific instructions
   299→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   300→    if instruction_fn:
   301→        parts.append(instruction_fn(mode))
   302→
   303→    # CLI reference
   304→    if mode == "self_record":
   305→        parts.append(render_cli_reference(cli_command))
   306→
   307→    # Validation requirements
   308→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   309→
   310→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   311→
   312→
   313→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   314→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   315→    *,
   316→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   317→) -> None:
   318→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   319→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   320→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   321→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   322→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   323→    state = runtime.state
   324→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   325→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   326→
   327→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   328→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   329→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   330→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   331→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   332→        if prior_stage == stage:
   333→            break
   334→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   335→        if report:
   336→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   337→
   338→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root, stages_data=stages)
   339→    print(prompt)
   340→
   341→
   342→__all__ = [
   343→    "build_observe_batch_prompt",
   344→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   345→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   346→    "build_stage_prompt",
   347→    "cmd_stage_prompt",
   348→    "_observe_batch_instructions",
   349→    "_validation_requirements",
   350→]
   351→
```

   103→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   104→
   105→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   106→
   107→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   108→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   109→
   110→**The Structured Observe Assessments table (provided below) is your primary input.** It contains
   111→a per-issue verdict (genuine/false-positive/exaggerated/over-engineering/not-worth-it) with reasoning. Use
   112→these verdicts as authoritative — do not second-guess observe unless you have specific evidence.
   113→
   114→**Important: Issues with verdict `false-positive` or `exaggerated` have already been auto-skipped
   115→by observe confirmation.** They are NOT in your issue set and do NOT need ledger entries.
   116→
   117→For issues with verdict `over-engineering` or `not-worth-it`: observe flagged these as
   118→questionable, but YOU decide. If you agree they're not worth fixing, skip them. If you disagree
   119→and think the fix has value, cluster them. These are judgment calls, not factual determinations.
   120→
   121→### What you must do:
   122→
   123→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from the observe assessments table)?
   124→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   125→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   126→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? Apply YAGNI: if the fix is more complex than the
   127→   problem, skip it. Valid skip reasons:
   128→   - "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication"
   129→   - "the current code is clear and simple despite being theoretically suboptimal"
   130→   - "observe verdict: not-worth-it — the improvement is marginal"
   131→   - "fixing this requires touching 8 files for a naming consistency issue nobody notices"
   132→   Invalid: "low priority" (everything is low priority compared to something)
   133→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   134→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   135→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   136→   another round of fixes.
   137→7. **Account for every issue exactly once** — every open issue hash must appear in exactly one
   138→   cluster line or one skip line. Do not drop hashes, and do not repeat a hash in multiple
   139→   clusters or in both a cluster and a skip.
   140→
   141→### Your report MUST include both a coverage ledger and a concrete cluster blueprint
   142→
   143→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   144→```
   145→## Coverage Ledger
   146→- a5996373 -> cluster "travel-structure-contract-unification"
   147→- fb113678 -> skip "false-positive-current-code"
   148→
   149→## Cluster Blueprint
   150→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks" (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   151→Cluster "task-typing" (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   152→
   153→## Skip Decisions
   154→Skip "false-positive-current-code" (false positive per observe)
   155→```
   156→
   157→### Hard accounting rule
   158→
   159→- Start your report with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.
   160→- In that section, mention each issue hash **once and only once** on its own ledger line.
   161→- Do **not** mention issue hashes again in cluster rationale paragraphs, recurring-pattern notes,
   162→  or ordering explanations. After the ledger, refer to clusters by name.
   163→- Before finishing, do a self-check: the ledger must cover all open issue hashes exactly once.
   164→
   165→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   166→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   167→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   168→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   169→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   170→- No `## Coverage Ledger`

   173→
   174→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   175→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   176→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   177→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   178→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).

   182→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."

   273→    subagent_block = """\
   274→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   275→

   306→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   307→
   308→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   309→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   310→
   311→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   312→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   313→Use the organize report included above as the authoritative cluster plan; do not re-derive
   314→strategy from old triage runs unless you find a concrete mismatch you need to explain.
   315→
   316→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   317→
   318→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   319→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   320→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   321→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)
   322→5. No step may reference a skipped/wontfixed issue in its issue_refs
   323→
   324→### How to enrich
   325→
   326→{subagent_block}
   327→
   328→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   329→
   330→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   331→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   332→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   333→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   334→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   335→
   336→**Vague action verbs.** "Refactor", "clean up", "improve", "fix" are not actions.
   337→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   338→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   339→
   340→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   341→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   342→
   343→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   344→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   345→
   346→**Over-engineering the fix.** KISS: the fix should be simpler than the problem. If you're
   347→adding interfaces, registries, factory methods, or configuration to fix a code smell, the
   348→cure is worse than the disease. Prefer: delete code > inline code > extract a function >
   349→add an abstraction. If the simplest fix is "delete these 10 lines," say that — don't
   350→propose a refactoring framework.
   351→
   352→### Examples
   353→
   354→**GOOD step detail:**
   355→```
   356→--detail "In src/shared/hooks/billing/useAutoTopup.ts lines 118-129, add onMutate handler
   357→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore
   358→previous state and change showToast from false to true."
   359→--issue-refs 79baeebf --effort small
   360→```
   361→
   362→**BAD step detail (will be rejected):**
   363→```
   364→--detail "Fix silent error swallowing"  # No file, no location, no action
   365→--detail "Decompose god-hooks"  # What file? What hooks? Into what?
   366→--detail "Address the issues identified in the observe stage"  # This says nothing
   367→```
   368→
   369→### Do NOT mark steps as done
   370→
   371→Use `--update-step N` to add detail, effort, and issue-refs.
   372→Do NOT use `--done-step` — steps are only marked done when actual code changes are made.
   373→
   374→### File path rules
   375→
   376→Only reference files that exist RIGHT NOW. Do not reference files that a step will create
   377→(e.g., a new shared module) or rename targets (the new filename after a rename). Reference
   378→the current source file and describe what will change. The path validator will block
   379→confirmation if paths don't exist on disk.
   380→
   381→{tail}
   382→"""
