   385→def _sense_check_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   386→    content_fix_block = (
   387→        'Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..." --effort <tag>`'
   388→    )
   389→    structure_fix_block = """\
   390→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other>`
   391→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "..." --detail "..." --effort trivial --issue-refs <hash>`
   392→"""
   393→    tail = """\
   394→When done, run:
   395→```
   396→desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<findings summary>"
   397→```
   398→"""
   399→    if mode == "output_only":
   400→        content_fix_block = (
   401→            "Report the exact step corrections that need to be made; the orchestrator will apply them."
   402→        )
   403→        structure_fix_block = (
   404→            "Report the exact dependency additions or cascade steps that need to be made; "
   405→            "the orchestrator will apply them.\n"
   406→        )
   407→        tail = """\
   408→When done, write a plain-text sense-check report with concrete content and structure fixes.
   409→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
   410→"""
   411→    investigation_hint = (
   412→        "Use `desloppify show <issue-hash> --no-budget` to verify step details "
   413→        "match the original reviewer analysis.\n\n"
   414→        if mode == "self_record"
   415→        else ""
   416→    )
   417→    constraints = render_constraints(header="   Also flag steps that:", bullet="   - ")
   418→    return f"""\
   419→## SENSE-CHECK Stage Instructions
   420→
   421→This stage is handled by two parallel subagents. If you are being run as a
   422→single-subprocess fallback, perform BOTH the content and structure checks below.
   423→
   424→### Content Check (per cluster)
   425→{investigation_hint}For EVERY step in every cluster, read the actual source file and verify:
   426→1. OVER-ENGINEERING (check FIRST — if the fix fails this, skip the rest):
   427→   Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:
   428→   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation
   429→   - Introduce unnecessary config/feature-flags/generalization
   430→   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit
   431→   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires
   432→   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)
   433→   - Add more lines of code than they remove (net complexity increase)
   434→{constraints}
   435→   Remove or simplify over-engineered steps. If the whole cluster is net-negative,
   436→   recommend removing it entirely — don't just flag it, say "skip this cluster."
   437→2. STALENESS: Is the problem still present, or already fixed?
   438→3. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match the step description?
   439→4. NAMES: Do function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?
   440→5. COUNTS: Are counts ("update the 3 imports") accurate?
   441→6. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step?
   442→7. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match actual scope?
   443→8. DUPLICATES: Flag steps that duplicate work in another cluster.
   444→
   445→{content_fix_block}
   446→
   447→### Structure Check (global)
   448→Build a file-touch graph and check:
   449→1. SHARED FILES: Two clusters touching same file without --depends-on → add dependency
   450→2. MISSING CASCADE: Rename/remove without importer updates → add cascade step
   451→3. CIRCULAR DEPS: Flag cycles, don't add them
   452→
   453→{structure_fix_block}
   454→
   455→{tail}
   456→"""
   457→
   458→
   459→_STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS = {
   460→    "observe": _observe_instructions,
   461→    "reflect": _reflect_instructions,
   462→    "organize": _organize_instructions,
   463→    "enrich": _enrich_instructions,
   464→    "sense-check": _sense_check_instructions,
   465→}

   471→    "_observe_instructions",
   472→    "_organize_instructions",
   473→    "_reflect_instructions",

   475→]

   135→def confirm_organize(
   136→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   137→    plan: dict,
   138→    stages: dict,
   139→    attestation: str | None,
   140→    *,
   141→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    """Show full plan summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   144→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   145→    if not ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages, stage="organize"):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   149→    state = runtime.state
   150→
   151→    print(colorize("  Stage: ORGANIZE — Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize", "bold"))
   152→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 63, "dim"))

   160→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   161→    ]
   162→    if not _require_enriched_clusters(plan):
   163→        return
   164→    if not _require_clustered_review_issues(plan, state):
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    _print_cluster_shape_warnings(plan)
   168→    all_clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   169→    _print_orphaned_cluster_notes(all_clusters)
   170→
   171→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state))
   172→    if not finalize_stage_confirmation(
   173→        plan=plan,
   174→        stages=stages,
   175→        request=StageConfirmationRequest(
   176→            stage="organize",
   177→            attestation=attestation,
   178→            min_attestation_len=MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
   179→            command_hint='desloppify plan triage --confirm organize --attestation "This plan is correct..."',
   180→            validation_stage="organize",
   181→            validate_attestation_fn=validate_attestation,
   182→            validation_kwargs={"cluster_names": organize_clusters},
   183→            log_action="triage_confirm_organize",
   184→            log_detail={"coverage": f"{organized}/{total}"},
   185→            not_satisfied_hint="If not, adjust clusters, priorities, or queue order before completing.",
   186→        ),
   187→        services=resolved_services,
   188→    ):
   189→        return
   190→    print_user_message(
   191→        "Hey — organize is confirmed. Next: enrich your steps"
   192→        " with detail and issue_refs so they're executor-ready."
   193→        " Run `desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report \"...\"`."
   194→        " You can still reorganize (add/remove clusters, reorder)"
   195→        " during the enrich stage."
   196→    )

Perfect! I've found the bug. Now let me create a comprehensive report of my findings.

## Bug Report: Unescaped Single Quotes in Triage Runner Shell Commands

I've found the quoting bug in the desloppify triage runner. Here's the complete analysis:

### Location
**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts_instruction_blocks.py`

**Line**: 201

### The Bug
In the `_organize_instructions()` function, the shell command is being generated with improperly escaped single quotes. The problematic code is:

```python
desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason from reflect>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."
```

When the subagent (Claude Code running as `--runner codex`) generates a shell command by interpolating `<pattern>` and `<reason from reflect>`, any single quotes in those values will break shell quoting. For example, if the reason is:

```
can't fix this
```

The generated command becomes:

```bash
desloppify plan skip --permanent pattern --note "can't fix this" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."
```

This triggers: `zsh: unmatched '` because the single quote inside the double-quoted `--note` argument is unescaped.

### Root Cause
The shell command template in line 201 does not properly escape or protect special characters (especially single quotes) that could appear in:
- Skip patterns (the `<pattern>` placeholder)
- Skip reasons (the `<reason from reflect>` placeholder)

### Why This Happens During "organize" Stage
The organize stage runs with `--runner codex`, which means Claude Code is executing as a subprocess. When Claude Code reads the prompt from `stage_prompts_instruction_blocks.py`, it gets this shell command template and tries to construct actual commands by substituting real values for `<pattern>` and `<reason from reflect>`. If those values contain single quotes, the command breaks.

### Affected Files
This is also present in other locations for reference:

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts_instruction_shared.py` (line 163) — but this is in the static CLI reference, not interpolated
2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/organize_policy.py` (line 119) — same pattern but in error message output, not shell execution
3. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/reporting/text.py` (lines 42, 44) — advisory text

### The Fix
The shell command template in `stage_prompts_instruction_blocks.py` line 201 needs to use a quoting mechanism that's safe for all character inputs. The pattern should use `--note` with proper escaping. A few options:

1. **Use single quotes for the whole command and escape embedded single quotes**:
   ```python
   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note '<reason from reflect>' --attest 'I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect.'
   ```
   But then single quotes in `<reason>` need escaping.

2. **Use printf-style quoting for shell safety** (recommended for subagent prompts):
   ```python
   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "$reason_from_reflect_escaped" --attest 'I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect.'
   ```

3. **Instruct the subagent to escape values** (most explicit):
   Add guidance in the organize stage instructions telling the subagent to escape single quotes in reasons by using `'"'"'` or similar shell escaping patterns.

The safest approach for a subprocess that will execute shell commands is option 3 combined with using single-quoted strings where possible, since single quotes are the most literal in shell.

    81→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    82→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    84→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    85→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:

    88→            return stage, True

    91→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    92→        if sid not in order:
    93→            continue
    94→        return stage, False
    95→
    96→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    97→        return "commit", False
    98→
    99→    return None, False

   110→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'

   112→    if stage == "commit":

   114→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)

   263→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   265→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   266→        return []
   267→    deferred: list[str] = []
   268→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   269→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   270→            continue
   271→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   272→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   273→    deferred.sort()
   274→    return deferred
   275→
   276→
   277→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   278→    plan: dict,
   279→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   280→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   281→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   282→    if not deferred_set:
   283→        return 0, 0
   284→
   285→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   286→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   287→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   288→
   289→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   290→    cluster_count = 0
   291→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   292→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   293→            continue
   294→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   295→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   296→            continue
   297→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   298→        if not matched:
   299→            continue
   300→        cluster_count += 1
   301→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   302→
   303→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   304→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   305→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
    10→    get_issue_description,
    11→    get_issue_note,
    12→    get_issue_override,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    15→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cluster_issue_ids(cluster: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    19→    """Return canonical cluster members after plan-load normalization."""
    20→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
    22→        return []
    23→    return [issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id]
    24→
    25→
    26→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    27→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    28→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    29→    if not scan_history:
    30→        return set()
    31→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    32→    if not threshold:
    33→        return set()
    34→    return {
    35→        issue_id
    36→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    37→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    42→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    43→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    44→
    45→    for item in items:
    46→        issue_id = item["id"]
    47→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    48→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    49→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    50→        if description:
    51→            item["plan_description"] = description
    52→        if note:
    53→            item["plan_note"] = note
    54→        if override.get("cluster"):
    55→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    56→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    57→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    58→                "name": cluster_name,
    59→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    60→                "total_items": len(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data)),
    61→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    62→            }
    63→
    64→
    65→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    66→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    67→    plan: dict,
    68→    new_ids: set[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    71→
    72→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    73→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    74→    """
    75→    queue_order = list(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    76→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order)
    77→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    78→
    79→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    80→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    81→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    82→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    83→
    84→    for item in items:
    85→        item_id = item["id"]
    86→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    87→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    88→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    89→
    90→
    91→def separate_skipped(
    92→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    93→    plan: dict,
    94→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    95→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    96→
    97→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    98→    """
    99→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return items, []
   102→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   103→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   104→    for item in items:
   105→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   106→            skipped.append(item)
   107→        else:
   108→            non_skipped.append(item)
   109→    return non_skipped, skipped
   110→
   111→
   112→def filter_cluster_focus(
   113→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   114→    plan: dict,
   115→    cluster: str | None,
   116→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   117→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   118→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   119→    if not effective_cluster:
   120→        return items
   121→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   122→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   123→    cluster_member_ids = set(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data))
   124→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   125→        return items
   126→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   127→
   128→
   129→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   131→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   132→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   133→
   134→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   135→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   136→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   137→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   138→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   139→            if skip_entry:
   140→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   141→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   142→                if skip_reason:
   143→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   144→
   145→
   146→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   147→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   148→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   149→    if meta:
   150→        return meta.action_type
   151→    return "manual_fix"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _build_cluster_meta(
   155→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   156→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   157→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   158→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   159→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   160→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   161→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   162→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   163→        action_type = "reorganize"
   164→    else:
   165→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   166→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   167→            action_type = "refactor"
   168→
   169→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   170→    total_in_cluster = len(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data))
   171→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   172→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   173→    else:
   174→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   175→
   176→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   177→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   178→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   179→        autofix_hint = action
   180→    else:
   181→        primary_command = action or f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   182→        autofix_hint = None
   183→
   184→    estimated_impact = max(
   185→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   186→    )
   187→
   188→    return {
   189→        "id": cluster_name,
   190→        "kind": "cluster",
   191→        "action_type": action_type,
   192→        "summary": summary,
   193→        "members": members,
   194→        "member_count": len(members),
   195→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   196→        "autofix_hint": autofix_hint,
   197→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   198→        "cluster_auto": bool(cluster_data.get("auto")),
   199→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   200→        "confidence": "high",
   201→        "detector": detector,
   202→        "file": "",
   203→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   204→        "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
   205→    }
   206→
   207→
   208→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   209→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   210→
   211→    Both auto-clusters and manual (triage) clusters are collapsed.  Manual
   212→    clusters are inserted at the front in plan order so triage-prioritised
   213→    work appears before auto-clustered mechanical items.
   214→    """
   215→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   216→    if not clusters:
   217→        return items
   218→
   219→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   220→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   221→        for issue_id in _cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   222→            # Manual clusters take priority when an issue is in both
   223→            if issue_id not in fid_to_cluster or not cluster.get("auto"):
   224→                fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   225→
   226→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   227→        return items
   228→
   229→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   230→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   231→    for item in items:
   232→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   233→        if cname:
   234→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   235→
   236→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   237→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   238→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   239→        if len(members) < 2:
   240→            continue
   241→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   242→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   243→        )
   244→
   245→    # Collect manual cluster names in plan order (for front-insertion)
   246→    manual_names = [
   247→        name for name in clusters
   248→        if not clusters[name].get("auto") and name in meta_items
   249→    ]
   250→
   251→    # Walk in order: replace first auto-cluster member with meta-item,
   252→    # skip subsequent members.  Manual cluster members are always skipped
   253→    # (they'll be inserted at the front).
   254→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   255→    rest: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   256→    for item in items:
   257→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   258→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   259→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   260→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   261→                # Auto-clusters collapse in-place; manual clusters go to front
   262→                if clusters.get(cname, {}).get("auto"):
   263→                    rest.append(meta_items[cname])
   264→            # skip individual member
   265→        else:
   266→            rest.append(item)
   267→
   268→    # Manual clusters at the front in plan order, then everything else
   269→    manual_result = [meta_items[name] for name in manual_names if name in seen_clusters]
   270→    return manual_result + rest
   271→
   272→
   273→__all__ = [
   274→    "collapse_clusters",
   275→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   276→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   277→    "new_item_ids",
   278→    "separate_skipped",
   279→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   280→    "stamp_positions",
   281→]
   282→
```

    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueSnapshot:
    46→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    47→
    48→    phase: str
    49→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]

    63→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    64→    triage_pending_count: int
    65→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool

    67→
    68→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    69→    if options is None:
    70→        return default
    71→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    75→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    76→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    77→        return state.get("scan_path")
    78→    return scan_path
    79→
    80→
    81→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    82→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    83→        return True
    84→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    85→    if not scores:
    86→        return True
    87→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    91→    return (
    92→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
    93→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    94→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    99→    return [
   100→        item for item in items
   101→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   106→    plan: dict | None,
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   109→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   111→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   112→        return review_issue_items
   113→
   114→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   115→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   116→        return []
   117→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   118→        return []
   119→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   120→        return []
   121→    return review_issue_items
   122→
   123→
   124→def _subjective_partitions(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   128→    threshold: float,
   129→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   130→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   131→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   132→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   133→    return initial, postflight
   134→
   135→
   136→def _workflow_partitions(
   137→    plan: dict | None,
   138→    state: StateModel,
   139→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   140→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   141→        return [], [], []
   142→    scan_items = [
   143→        item
   144→        for item in (
   145→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   146→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   147→        )
   148→        if item is not None
   149→    ]
   150→    postflight_workflow = [
   151→        item
   152→        for item in (
   153→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   154→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   155→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   156→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   157→        )
   158→        if item is not None
   159→    ]
   160→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   161→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   162→
   163→
   164→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   165→    *,
   166→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   167→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   168→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   169→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   170→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   171→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   172→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   173→    triage_blocks_execution: bool,
   174→) -> str:
   175→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   176→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   177→    if objective_items and not triage_blocks_execution:
   178→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   179→    if scan_items:
   180→        return PHASE_SCAN
   181→    if triage_blocks_execution and postflight_workflow_items:
   182→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   183→    if triage_blocks_execution and triage_items:
   184→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   185→    if objective_items:
   186→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   187→    if postflight_review_items:
   188→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   189→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   190→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   191→    if triage_items:
   192→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   193→    return PHASE_SCAN
   194→
   195→
   196→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   197→    phase: str,
   198→    *,
   199→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   200→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   201→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   202→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   203→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   204→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   205→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   206→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   207→        return initial_review_items
   208→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   209→        return objective_items
   210→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   211→        deferred_items = [
   212→            item for item in scan_items
   213→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   214→        ]
   215→        if deferred_items:
   216→            return deferred_items
   217→        return [
   218→            item for item in scan_items
   219→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   220→        ]
   221→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   222→        return postflight_review_items
   223→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   224→        return postflight_workflow_items
   225→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   226→        return triage_items
   227→    return []
   228→
   229→
   230→def build_queue_snapshot(
   231→    state: StateModel,
   232→    *,
   233→    options: object | None = None,
   234→    plan: dict | None = None,
   235→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   236→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   237→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   238→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   239→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   240→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   241→    )
   242→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   243→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   244→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   245→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   246→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   247→
   248→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   249→        state,
   250→        scan_path=scan_path,
   251→        status_filter="open",
   252→        scope=scope,
   253→        chronic=chronic,
   254→    )
   255→    objective_items = [
   256→        item for item in all_issue_items
   257→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   258→    ]
   259→    planned_ids = _planned_objective_ids(
   260→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   261→        effective_plan,
   262→    )
   263→    planned_objective_items = [
   264→        item for item in objective_items
   265→        if item.get("id", "") in planned_ids
   266→    ]
   267→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   268→    triage_snapshot = (
   269→        build_triage_snapshot(effective_plan, state)
   270→        if isinstance(effective_plan, dict)
   271→        else None
   272→    )
   273→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   274→        effective_plan,
   275→        state,
   276→        review_issue_items,
   277→    )
   278→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   279→        state,
   280→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   281→        threshold=target_strict,
   282→    )
   283→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *executable_review_items]
   284→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   285→        effective_plan,
   286→        state,
   287→    )
   288→
   289→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   290→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   291→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   292→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   293→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   294→        scan_items=scan_items,
   295→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   296→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   297→        triage_items=triage_items,
   298→        triage_blocks_execution=bool(
   299→            review_issue_items
   300→            and triage_snapshot is not None
   301→            and triage_snapshot.requires_plan_before_execution
   302→        ),
   303→    )
   304→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   305→        phase,
   306→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   307→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   308→        scan_items=scan_items,
   309→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   310→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   311→        triage_items=triage_items,
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   315→    backlog_items = [
   316→        item for item in (
   317→            [
   318→                *planned_objective_items,
   319→                *initial_review_items,
   320→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   321→                *review_issue_items,
   322→                *scan_items,
   323→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   324→                *triage_items,
   325→            ]
   326→        )
   327→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   328→    ]
   329→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   330→        1 for item in planned_objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   331→    )
   332→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(planned_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   333→
   334→    return QueueSnapshot(
   335→        phase=phase,
   336→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   337→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   338→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   339→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   340→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   341→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   342→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   343→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   344→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   345→        planned_objective_count=len(planned_objective_items),
   346→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   347→            1
   348→            for item in execution_items
   349→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   350→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   351→        ),
   352→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   353→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   354→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   355→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   356→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   357→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   358→    )
   359→
   360→
   361→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   362→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   363→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   364→        return "review"
   365→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   366→        return "workflow"
   367→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   368→        return "triage"
   369→    return phase
   370→
   371→
   372→__all__ = [
   373→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   374→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   375→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   376→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   377→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   378→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   379→    "QueueSnapshot",
   380→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   381→    "coarse_phase_name",
   382→]
   383→

    44→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    45→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    46→
    47→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    48→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    49→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    50→    """
    51→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    52→    synthetic = [item for item in order if any(item.startswith(p) for p in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)]
    53→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    54→        return False
    55→    for item in synthetic:
    56→        order.remove(item)
    57→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    58→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    59→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    60→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    61→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    62→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    63→    count = len(synthetic)
    64→    if count:
    65→        print(
    66→            colorize(
    67→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    68→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    69→                "yellow",
    70→            )
    71→        )
    72→    return True
    73→
    74→
    75→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    76→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    77→    return bool(
    78→        plan.get("queue_order")
    79→        or plan.get("overrides")
    80→        or plan.get("clusters")
    81→        or plan.get("skipped")
    82→    )

    85→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    86→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    87→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    88→        return False
    89→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    90→    if recon.resurfaced:
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(
    93→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    94→                "cyan",
    95→            )
    96→        )
    97→    return bool(recon.changes)

   127→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   128→    plan: dict[str, object],
   129→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   130→    *,
   131→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   132→    policy=None,

   142→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   143→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   144→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   145→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   146→        return False
   147→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   148→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   149→        return False
   150→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   151→    if scores.strict is None:
   152→        return False
   153→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   154→        "strict": scores.strict,
   155→        "overall": scores.overall,
   156→        "objective": scores.objective,
   157→        "verified": scores.verified,
   158→    }
   159→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   160→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   161→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   162→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   163→    return True
   164→
   165→
   166→def _has_objective_cycle(
   167→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   168→    plan: dict[str, object],
   169→) -> bool | None:
   170→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   171→    try:
   172→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   173→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   174→        )
   175→
   176→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   177→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   178→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   179→        return None
   180→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   181→
   182→
   183→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   184→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   185→) -> bool:
   186→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   187→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   188→        return False
   189→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   190→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   191→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   192→        return False
   193→
   194→    try:
   195→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   196→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   197→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   198→            score_display_mode,
   199→        )
   200→
   201→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   202→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   203→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   204→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   205→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   206→        return False
   207→    if not queue_empty:
   208→        return False
   209→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   210→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   211→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   212→    # in the next cycle.
   213→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   214→    return True

   217→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(

   222→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:

   225→    if objective_cycle is not False:

   450→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   451→    *,
   452→    plan: dict[str, object],
   453→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   454→) -> bool:
   455→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   456→    return bool(_apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan))
   457→
   458→
   459→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   460→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   461→
   462→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   463→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   464→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   465→
   466→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   467→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   468→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   469→    """
   470→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   471→        return False
   472→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   473→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   474→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   475→
   476→
   477→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   478→    *,
   479→    plan: dict[str, object],
   480→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   481→    target_strict: float,
   482→    policy,
   483→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   484→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   485→) -> bool:
   486→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   487→
   488→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   489→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   490→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   491→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   492→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   493→    """
   494→    dirty = False
   495→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   496→
   497→    if _sync_subjective_and_log(
   498→        plan,
   499→        state,
   500→        policy=policy,
   501→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   502→    ):
   503→        dirty = True
   504→    if not mid_cycle:
   505→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   506→            plan,
   507→            state,
   508→            target_strict=target_strict,
   509→            policy=policy,
   510→        ):
   511→            dirty = True
   512→    else:
   513→        print(
   514→            colorize(
   515→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   516→                "preserve queue state.",
   517→                "dim",
   518→            )
   519→        )
   520→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   521→        dirty = True
   522→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   523→        dirty = True
   524→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   525→        dirty = True
   526→    if not force_rescan:
   527→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   528→            dirty = True
   529→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   530→            dirty = True
   531→    if _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state):
   532→        dirty = True
   533→    return dirty
   534→
   535→
   536→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   537→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   538→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   539→    try:
   540→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   541→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   542→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   543→        return
   544→
   545→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   546→
   547→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   548→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   549→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   550→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   551→
   552→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   553→
   554→    # Policy must be computed after reconciliation, which mutates plan
   555→    # (supersede/prune resolved issues) before policy reads it.
   556→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   557→        runtime,
   558→        plan,
   559→    )
   560→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   561→        plan=plan,
   562→        state=runtime.state,
   563→        target_strict=target_strict,
   564→        policy=policy,
   565→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   566→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   567→    ) or dirty
   568→
   569→    if dirty:
   570→        try:
   571→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   572→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   573→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   574→
```

   283→def _resolve_queue_items(
   284→    *,
   285→    state: dict,
   286→    config: dict,
   287→    opts: NextOptions,
   288→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   289→    target_strict: float,
   290→    build_work_queue_fn,
   291→) -> tuple[object, dict, list[dict], str | None]:
   292→    ctx = queue_context(
   293→        state,
   294→        target_strict=target_strict,
   295→        config=config,
   296→        plan=plan_for_queue,
   297→    )
   298→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   299→        plan_for_queue,
   300→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   301→        scope=opts.scope,
   302→    )
   303→    queue = build_work_queue_fn(
   304→        state,
   305→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   306→            count=None,
   307→            scope=opts.scope,
   308→            status=opts.status,
   309→            include_subjective=True,
   310→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   311→            explain=opts.explain,
   312→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   313→            context=ctx,
   314→        ),
   315→    )
   316→    return ctx, queue, queue.get("items", []), effective_cluster
   317→
   318→
   319→def _apply_queue_view_transforms(
   320→    *,
   321→    items: list[dict],
   322→    queue: dict,
   323→    opts: NextOptions,
   324→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   325→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   326→    collapse_plan_clusters: bool,
   327→) -> list[dict]:
   328→    visible = items
   329→    if collapse_plan_clusters and effective_cluster and plan_for_queue:
   330→        visible = filter_cluster_focus(visible, plan_for_queue, effective_cluster)
   331→    elif (
   332→        collapse_plan_clusters
   333→        and plan_for_queue
   334→        and not effective_cluster
   335→        and not plan_for_queue.get("active_cluster")
   336→    ):
   337→        visible = collapse_clusters(visible, plan_for_queue)
   338→
   339→    if opts.count:
   340→        visible = visible[: opts.count]
   341→        queue["items"] = visible
   342→        queue["total"] = len(visible)
   343→    return visible

   346→def _render_non_empty_queue(

   348→    args: argparse.Namespace,

   365→    narrative = compute_narrative(

   378→    )
   379→
   380→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items, command_name=view.command_name):
   381→        return
   382→
   383→    _render_terminal_queue_view(

   399→def _build_and_render_queue_view(
   400→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   401→    state: dict,
   402→    config: dict,
   403→    *,
   404→    deps: QueueRenderDeps,
   405→    view: QueueViewConfig,
   406→) -> None:
   407→    """Build queue payload and render output for a queue surface."""
   408→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   409→    guardrail_warnings = triage_guardrail_messages(state=state)
   410→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   411→
   412→    plan = deps.load_plan_fn()
   413→    plan_for_queue = plan
   414→    plan_data = _plan_data_for_view(plan, show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context)
   415→    ctx, queue, items, effective_cluster = _resolve_queue_items(
   416→        state=state,
   417→        config=config,
   418→        opts=opts,
   419→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,

   421→        build_work_queue_fn=deps.build_work_queue_fn,
   422→    )
   423→    items = _apply_queue_view_transforms(

   425→        queue=queue,

   427→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   428→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   429→        collapse_plan_clusters=view.collapse_plan_clusters,
   430→    )
   431→
   432→    if not items:

   435→            items=items,
   436→            state=state,
   437→            plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   438→            plan_data=plan_data,
   439→            ctx=ctx,
   440→            target_strict=target_strict,
   441→            opts=opts,
   442→            guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   443→            write_query_fn=deps.write_query_fn,
   444→            command_name=view.command_name,
   445→            show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context,
   446→        )
   447→        return
   448→
   449→    _render_non_empty_queue(
   450→        args=args,
   451→        state=state,
   452→        items=items,
   453→        queue=queue,
   454→        plan_data=plan_data,

   458→        opts=opts,
   459→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   460→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   461→        target_strict=target_strict,
   462→        ctx=ctx,
   463→        resolve_lang_fn=deps.resolve_lang_fn,
   464→    )

    19→def _subjective_threshold(state: StateModel, *, default: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE) -> float:
    20→    config = state.get("config", {})
    21→    raw_target = default
    22→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    23→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    24→    try:
    25→        value = float(raw_target)
    26→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    27→        value = default
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _queue_plan_from_options(options: QueueBuildOptions) -> dict | None:
    32→    if options.context is not None:
    33→        return options.context.plan
    34→    return options.plan
    35→
    36→
    37→def build_open_plan_queue(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    41→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    42→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    43→    opts = replace(options) if options is not None else QueueBuildOptions(count=None)
    44→    if options is None:
    45→        opts = replace(opts, status="open", include_subjective=True)
    46→    elif opts.status == QueueBuildOptions().status:
    47→        opts = replace(opts, status="open")
    48→    if opts.subjective_threshold == QueueBuildOptions().subjective_threshold:
    49→        opts = replace(opts, subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state))
    50→    return build_work_queue(
    51→        state,
    52→        options=opts,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def build_execution_queue(
    57→    state: StateModel,
    58→    *,
    59→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    60→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    61→    """Build the execution queue that follows the living plan when present."""
    62→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    63→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    64→        state,
    65→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    66→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    67→    )
    68→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    69→        state,
    70→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    71→        visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def build_backlog_queue(
    76→    state: StateModel,
    77→    *,
    78→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    79→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    80→    """Build the broader backlog, excluding work already tracked in the plan."""
    81→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    82→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    83→        state,
    84→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    85→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    86→    )
    87→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    88→        state,
    89→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    90→        visibility=QueueVisibility.BACKLOG,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "build_backlog_queue",
    96→    "build_execution_queue",
    97→    "build_open_plan_queue",

1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→
    16→[REDACTED]
    17→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    18→
    19→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    24→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    25→    {
    26→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    31→    }
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    37→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    38→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    39→        refresh_state = {}
    40→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    41→    return refresh_state
    42→
    43→
    44→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    45→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    46→
    47→
    48→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    49→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    50→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    51→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    52→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    53→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    54→            return phase
    55→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    56→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    57→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    59→    return None
    60→
    61→
    62→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    63→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    64→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    65→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    66→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
    67→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
    68→        return False
    69→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
    70→    return True
    71→
    72→
    73→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
    74→    plan: PlanModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
    77→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
    78→    has_postflight_review: bool,
    79→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
    80→    has_triage: bool,
    81→    has_deferred: bool,
    82→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    83→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
    84→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    85→        plan,
    86→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
    87→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
    88→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
    89→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
    90→        has_triage=has_triage,
    91→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
    92→    )
    93→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    94→
    95→
    96→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    97→    plan: PlanModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   100→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   101→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   102→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   103→    has_triage: bool,
   104→    has_deferred: bool,
   105→) -> str:
   106→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   107→    if has_initial_reviews:
   108→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   109→    if has_objective_backlog:
   110→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   111→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   112→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   113→    if has_postflight_review:
   114→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   115→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   116→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   117→    if has_triage:
   118→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   119→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   120→    if persisted is not None:
   121→        return persisted
   122→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   123→
   124→
   125→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   126→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   127→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   128→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   129→        return True
   130→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   131→
   132→
   133→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   134→    plan: PlanModel,
   135→    *,
   136→    scan_count: int | None,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   139→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   140→    try:
   141→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   142→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   143→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   144→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   145→        return False
   146→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   147→    return True
   148→
   149→
   150→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on real issues."""
   156→    if issue_ids is not None and not any(
   157→        _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids
   158→    ):
   159→        return False
   160→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   161→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   162→        return False
   163→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   164→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   165→    return True
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   170→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   171→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",
   172→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW",
   173→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN",
   174→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE",
   175→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW",
   176→    "mark_postflight_scan_completed",
   177→    "postflight_scan_pending",
   178→    "resolve_lifecycle_phase",
   179→    "set_lifecycle_phase",
   180→    "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   181→    "VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES",
   182→]
   183→

    19→def build_work_queue(

    31→
    32→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    33→    state: StateModel,
    34→    *,
    35→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    36→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    39→
    40→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    41→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    42→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.

    46→    items = select_queue_items(

    51→        status=status,
    52→        threshold=threshold,
    53→        visibility=visibility,
    54→    )
    55→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)

1→"""Queue item selection from canonical snapshot or raw issue lists."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import gather_subjective_items
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, QueueVisibility
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import build_queue_snapshot
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→
    14→def select_queue_items(
    15→    state: StateModel,
    16→    *,
    17→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    18→    plan: dict | None,
    19→    scan_path: str | None,
    20→    status: str,
    21→    threshold: float,
    22→    visibility: str,
    23→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    24→    """Select the raw queue item list before ranking/finalization."""
    25→    if status == "open" and not opts.chronic:
    26→        snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=opts,
    29→            plan=plan,
    30→            target_strict=threshold,
    31→        )
    32→        return filter_snapshot_items(
    33→            items_for_visibility(snapshot=snapshot, visibility=visibility),
    34→            opts,
    35→        )
    36→
    37→    items = build_issue_items(
    38→        state,
    39→        scan_path=scan_path,
    40→        status_filter=status,
    41→        scope=opts.scope,
    42→        chronic=opts.chronic,
    43→    )
    44→    if opts.include_subjective and status == "all":
    45→        items += gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
    46→    return items
    47→
    48→
    49→def items_for_visibility(*, snapshot, visibility: str) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    50→    """Select the snapshot partition for one queue surface."""
    51→    if visibility == QueueVisibility.BACKLOG:
    52→        return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.backlog_items]
    53→    return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.execution_items]
    54→
    55→
    56→def filter_snapshot_items(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    59→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    60→    """Apply view-local filtering after snapshot partition selection."""
    61→    filtered = items
    62→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    63→        filtered = [
    64→            item for item in filtered
    65→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
    66→        ]
    67→    if opts.scope:
    68→        filtered = [
    69→            item for item in filtered
    70→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension" or scope_matches(item, opts.scope)
    71→        ]
    72→    return filtered
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = [
    76→    "filter_snapshot_items",
    77→    "items_for_visibility",
    78→    "select_queue_items",
    79→]

1→"""Queue ranking and plan-aware finalization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, WorkQueueResult
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
     7→    enrich_plan_metadata,
     8→    separate_skipped,
     9→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    10→    stamp_positions,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import new_item_ids as _new_item_ids
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    14→    enrich_with_impact,
    15→    group_queue_items,
    16→    item_explain,
    17→    item_sort_key,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    21→
    22→
    23→def finalize_queue(
    24→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    25→    *,
    26→    state: StateModel,
    27→    plan: dict | None,
    28→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    29→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    30→    """Apply ranking, plan ordering, limits, and explain metadata."""
    31→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    32→
    33→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
    34→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    35→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
    36→
    37→    total = len(items)
    38→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
    39→        items = items[:opts.count]
    40→    if opts.explain:
    41→        for item in items:
    42→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
    43→
    44→    return {
    45→        "items": items,
    46→        "total": total,
    47→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
    48→        "new_ids": new_ids,
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→def _plan_presort(
    53→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    54→    state: StateModel,
    55→    plan: dict | None,
    56→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    57→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting."""
    58→    if not plan:
    59→        return set(), []
    60→
    61→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
    62→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
    63→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
    64→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
    65→    items[:] = remaining
    66→    return new_ids, skipped
    67→
    68→
    69→def _plan_postsort(
    70→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    71→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
    72→    plan: dict | None,
    73→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions."""
    76→    if not plan:
    77→        return
    78→    if opts.include_skipped:
    79→        items.extend(skipped)
    80→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = ["finalize_queue"]
    84→
