```
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Dimension key normalization
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    51→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    52→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    53→    target = cleaned.lower()
    54→
    55→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    56→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    57→            return str(dim_key)
    58→    return slugify(cleaned)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    63→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    64→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    65→    return {
    66→        cleaned.lower(),
    67→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    68→        slugify(cleaned),
    69→        canonical.lower(),
    70→        slugify(canonical),
    71→    }
    72→
    73→
    74→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    75→# Subjective strict scores
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→
    78→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    79→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    80→    if not dim_scores:
    81→        return {}
    82→
    83→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    84→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    85→    for entry in entries:
    86→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    87→        if not name:
    88→            continue
    89→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    90→        [REDACTED](name)
    91→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    92→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    93→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    94→            if not key:
    95→                continue
    96→            aliases.add(key)
    97→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    98→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    99→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   100→        for alias in aliases:
   101→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   102→    return scores
   103→
   104→
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→# Synthetic item builders
   107→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108→
   109→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   111→
   112→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   113→    """
   114→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→    order_set = set(order)
   116→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   117→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   118→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   119→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    present_names = {
   121→        name
   122→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   123→        if sid in present_ids
   124→    }
   125→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   126→    if not present_names:
   127→        return []
   128→
   129→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   130→    open_review_count = sum(
   131→        1 for f in issues.values()
   132→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   133→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   134→    )
   135→
   136→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   137→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   138→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   139→        if name not in present_names:
   140→            continue
   141→        if name in confirmed:
   142→            continue
   143→
   144→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   145→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   146→        blocked_by = sorted(
   147→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   148→        )
   149→
   150→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   151→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   152→
   153→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   154→            "id": sid,
   155→            "tier": 1,
   156→            "confidence": "high",
   157→            "detector": "triage",
   158→            "file": ".",
   159→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   160→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   161→            "detail": {
   162→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   163→                "stage": name,
   164→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   165→                "runner_commands": [
   166→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   167→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   168→                ],
   169→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   170→            },
   171→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   172→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   173→        }
   174→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   175→        items.append(item)
   176→    return items
   177→
   178→
   179→def build_subjective_items(
   180→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   181→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   182→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   183→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   184→    if not dim_scores:
   185→        return []
   186→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   187→
   188→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   189→    if not subjective_entries:
   190→        return []
   191→    unassessed_dims = {
   192→        str(name).strip()
   193→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   194→            dim_scores
   195→        )
   196→    }
   197→
   198→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   199→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   200→    for issue in issues.values():
   201→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   202→            continue
   203→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   204→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   205→            if dim_key:
   206→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   207→
   208→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   209→    def _prepare_command(
   210→        cli_keys: list[str],
   211→        *,
   212→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   213→    ) -> str:
   214→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   215→        if cli_keys:
   216→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   217→        if force_review_rerun:
   218→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   219→        return command
   220→
   221→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   222→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   223→        if not name:
   224→            continue
   225→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   226→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   227→            continue
   228→
   229→        [REDACTED](name)
   230→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   231→        cli_keys = [
   232→            str(key).strip().lower()
   233→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   234→            if str(key).strip()
   235→        ]
   236→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   237→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   238→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   239→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   240→            name in unassessed_dims
   241→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   242→        )
   243→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   244→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   245→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   246→        if open_review > 0:
   247→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   248→        else:
   249→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   250→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   251→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   252→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   253→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   254→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   255→            "file": ".",
   256→            "confidence": "medium",
   257→            "summary": summary,
   258→            "detail": {
   259→                "dimension_name": name,
   260→                "dimension": dim_key,
   261→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   262→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   263→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   264→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   265→            },
   266→            "status": "open",
   267→        }
   268→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   269→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   270→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   271→        items.append(item)
   272→    return items
   273→
   274→
   275→__all__ = [
   276→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   277→    "build_create_plan_item",
   278→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   279→    "build_import_scores_item",
   280→    "build_run_scan_item",
   281→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   282→    "build_subjective_items",
   283→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   284→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   285→]
   286→
```

```
   136→def _workflow_partitions(
   137→    plan: dict | None,
   138→    state: StateModel,
   139→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   140→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   141→        return [], [], []
   142→    scan_items = [
   143→        item
   144→        for item in (
   145→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   146→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   147→        )
   148→        if item is not None
   149→    ]
   150→    postflight_workflow = [
   151→        item
   152→        for item in (
   153→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   154→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   155→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   156→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   157→        )
   158→        if item is not None
   159→    ]
   160→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   161→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   162→
   163→
   164→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   165→    *,
   166→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   167→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   168→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   169→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   170→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   171→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   172→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   173→    triage_blocks_execution: bool,
   174→) -> str:
   175→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   176→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   177→    if objective_items and not triage_blocks_execution:
   178→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   179→    if scan_items:
   180→        return PHASE_SCAN
   181→    if triage_blocks_execution and postflight_workflow_items:
   182→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   183→    if triage_blocks_execution and triage_items:
   184→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   185→    if objective_items:
   186→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   187→    if postflight_review_items:
   188→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   189→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   190→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   191→    if triage_items:
   192→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   193→    return PHASE_SCAN
   194→
   195→
   196→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   197→    phase: str,
   198→    *,
   199→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   200→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   201→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   202→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   203→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   204→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   205→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   206→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   207→        return initial_review_items
   208→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   209→        return objective_items
   210→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   211→        deferred_items = [
   212→            item for item in scan_items
   213→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   214→        ]
   215→        if deferred_items:
   216→            return deferred_items
   217→        return [
   218→            item for item in scan_items
   219→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   220→        ]
   221→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   222→        return postflight_review_items
   223→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   224→        return postflight_workflow_items
   225→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   226→        return triage_items
   227→    return []
   228→
   229→
   230→def build_queue_snapshot(
   231→    state: StateModel,
   232→    *,
   233→    options: object | None = None,
   234→    plan: dict | None = None,
```

```
   298→        triage_blocks_execution=bool(
   299→            review_issue_items
   300→            and triage_snapshot is not None
   301→            and triage_snapshot.requires_plan_before_execution
   302→        ),
   303→    )
   304→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   305→        phase,
   306→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   307→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   308→        scan_items=scan_items,
   309→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   310→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   311→        triage_items=triage_items,
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   315→    backlog_items = [
   316→        item for item in (
   317→            [
   318→                *planned_objective_items,
   319→                *initial_review_items,
   320→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   321→                *review_issue_items,
   322→                *scan_items,
   323→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   324→                *triage_items,
   325→            ]
   326→        )
   327→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   328→    ]
   329→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   330→        1 for item in planned_objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   331→    )
   332→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(planned_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   333→
   334→    return QueueSnapshot(
   335→        phase=phase,
   336→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   337→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   338→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   339→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   340→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   341→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   342→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   343→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   344→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   345→        planned_objective_count=len(planned_objective_items),
   346→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   347→            1
   348→            for item in execution_items
   349→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   350→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   351→        ),
   352→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   353→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   354→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   355→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   356→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   357→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   358→    )
```

Perfect! Now I have the complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive analysis document:

## Analysis Summary

I've thoroughly reviewed the work queue and synthetic workflow code. Here's what I found:

### **The Problem: Scan Items Appearing After Triage**

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here's exactly how the work queue is constructed and why scan items might appear after triage stages:

#### **1. Queue Construction Flow**

The work queue is built in this order (from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`):

1. **Partition extraction** (`_workflow_partitions()`, lines 136-161):
   - Calls `build_run_scan_item(plan)` → returns scan item if `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` is True
   - Calls `build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)` → returns all triage stages in `queue_order`
   - Calls workflow builders for postflight items (checkpoint, import, create-plan, communicate-score)

2. **Phase resolution** (`_phase_for_snapshot()`, lines 164-193):
   - Determines which partition should execute based on what's present
   - **Critical logic** (lines 179-184):
     ```
     if scan_items:
         return PHASE_SCAN
     if triage_blocks_execution and postflight_workflow_items:
         return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
     if triage_blocks_execution and triage_items:
         return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
     ```
   - Scan phase takes **priority** (line 179) — scan items block triage execution

3. **Execution item selection** (`_execution_items_for_phase()`, lines 196-227):
   - For `PHASE_SCAN`: returns only scan items (deferred disposition or run-scan)
   - For `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`: returns only triage items
   - For `PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT`: returns only workflow items

#### **2. When Scan Items Appear**

Scan items are built by `build_run_scan_item(plan)` (lines 246-260 in synthetic_workflow.py):
- **Returns a `workflow::run-scan` item** when `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` is True
- **Returns `None`** when `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` is False

The `postflight_scan_pending()` function (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`, lines 125-130):
```python
def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
        return True  # Default: scan is pending
    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
```

**Key issue**: If `refresh_state.postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count` is **not set**, the function returns `True`, meaning a scan item is created even after triage has been injected.

#### **3. The Ordering Problem**

In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py` at lines 315-328, the **backlog** includes all items from all partitions:
```python
backlog_items = [
    item for item in (
        [
            *planned_objective_items,
            *initial_review_items,
            *subjective_postflight_items,
            *review_issue_items,
            *scan_items,           # <-- SCAN ITEMS IN BACKLOG
            *postflight_workflow_items,
            *triage_items,         # <-- TRIAGE ITEMS IN BACKLOG
        ]
    )
    if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids  # items not currently executing
]
```

This means:
- When `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT` is active, triage items execute
- But if `postflight_scan_pending()` mistakenly returns `True`, a scan item is also in the backlog
- The scan item appears **before** triage items in the backlog list because scan items are added to the array first (line 322 before line 324)

#### **4. Root Causes**

The scan item appears as a queue item after triage when:

1. **`postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count` is not properly set** after a scan completes
   - See `mark_postflight_scan_completed()` in refresh_lifecycle.py (lines 133-147)
   - This should be called in `plan_reconcile.py` after scan reconciliation completes

2. **Scan item is part of backlog, not execution** when phase is TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   - The scan item exists in `all_scan_items` but isn't in `execution_items`
   - When displayed, it appears after triage items because of how queue items are accumulated

3. **The queue partitioning logic doesn't enforce mutual exclusion**
   - Scan items, triage items, and workflow items can all exist simultaneously
   - The phase determines what **executes** but doesn't prevent others from being **queued**

#### **5. Expected Behavior**

According to the test file `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/review/test_work_queue_synthetic_workflow_direct.py` (lines 15-30):
- `build_run_scan_item()` should return a scan item **only when postflight_scan_pending() is True**
- Once a scan completes, `postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count` should be set
- Then `build_run_scan_item()` returns `None`, preventing the scan item from appearing

### **Key Files Involved**

1. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`** — Phase and partition logic
2. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py`** — Scan item builder
3. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`** — Scan completion tracking
4. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`** — Post-scan state synchronization
5. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/queue_flow.py`** — Queue rendering for `desloppify next`

### **The Fix**

The issue is likely in `plan_reconcile.py` where `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready()` (lines 217-230) determines whether to mark the scan as complete. It may not be:
- Detecting the right boundary condition for scan completion
- Properly calling `mark_postflight_scan_completed()` to set the sentinel value
- This causes `postflight_scan_pending()` to keep returning `True`, and scan items keep appearing in the queue

```
   102→def _emit_requested_output(
   103→    opts: NextOptions,
   104→    payload: dict,

   118→            return True
   119→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   120→
   121→    return next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(
   122→        opts.output_format,
   123→        payload,
   124→        items,
   125→        command=command_name,
   126→    )
   127→
   128→
   129→def _write_next_payload(
   130→    *,
   131→    queue: dict,
   132→    items: list[dict],
   133→    state: dict,

   135→    plan_data: dict | None,

   155→def _render_empty_queue_view(
   156→    *,
   157→    queue: dict,

   159→    state: dict,
   160→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   161→    plan_data: dict | None,
   162→    ctx,

   166→    write_query_fn,

   169→) -> None:

   171→    strict_score = score_snapshot(state).strict
   172→    plan_start_strict = None
   173→    if show_plan_context and plan_for_queue:

   179→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)

   196→
   197→
   198→def _render_terminal_queue_view(
   199→    *,
   200→    queue: dict,
   201→    items: list[dict],
   202→    state: dict,
   203→    opts: NextOptions,
   204→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   205→    plan_data: dict | None,
   206→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   207→    target_strict: float,
   208→    ctx,
   209→    show_plan_context: bool,
   210→    show_execution_prompt: bool,
   211→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   212→) -> None:
   213→    """Render terminal output for a non-empty queue."""
   214→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   215→    issues_scoped = path_scoped_issues(
   216→        state.get("issues", {}),
   217→        state.get("scan_path"),
   218→    )
   219→    plan_start_strict = None
   220→    breakdown = None
   221→    if show_plan_context:
   222→        plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   223→            state=state,
   224→            plan_data=plan_for_queue,
   225→            context=ctx,
   226→        )
   227→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   228→
   229→    _render_queue_header(queue, opts.explain)
   230→    for warning in guardrail_warnings:
   231→        print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
   232→    strict_score = score_snapshot(state).strict
   233→    if _show_empty_queue(
   234→        queue,
   235→        strict_score,
   236→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   237→        target_strict=target_strict,
   238→    ):
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(state.get("potentials", {}))
   242→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   243→        items,
   244→        dim_scores,
   245→        issues_scoped,
   246→        group=opts.group,
   247→        explain=opts.explain,
   248→        potentials=potentials,
   249→        plan=plan_data,
   250→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   251→    )
   252→    next_nudges_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   253→    if show_plan_context:
   254→        next_nudges_mod.render_uncommitted_reminder(plan_data)
   255→        next_nudges_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   256→            state,
   257→            dim_scores,
   258→            issues_scoped,
   259→            strict_score=strict_score,
   260→            target_strict_score=target_strict,
   261→            queue_total=queue_total,
   262→            plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   263→            breakdown=breakdown,
   264→        )

   266→
   267→    if items and plan_data and show_execution_prompt:
   268→        print_user_message(

   270→            " `desloppify plan resolve`. Full queue:"
   271→            " `desloppify plan show`."
   272→        )

   275→def _plan_data_for_view(plan: dict, *, show_plan_context: bool) -> dict | None:
   276→    if not show_plan_context:
   277→        return None
   278→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
   279→        return plan
   280→    return None

   285→    state: dict,
   286→    config: dict,
   287→    opts: NextOptions,

   289→    target_strict: float,

   291→) -> tuple[object, dict, list[dict], str | None]:
   292→    ctx = queue_context(
   293→        state,

   295→        config=config,
   296→        plan=plan_for_queue,
   297→    )
   298→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   299→        plan_for_queue,
   300→        cluster_arg=opts.cluster,
   301→        scope=opts.scope,
   302→    )
   303→    queue = build_work_queue_fn(
   304→        state,
   305→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   306→            count=None,
   307→            scope=opts.scope,
   308→            status=opts.status,
   309→            include_subjective=True,
   310→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   311→            explain=opts.explain,
   312→            include_skipped=opts.include_skipped,
   313→            context=ctx,
   314→        ),
   315→    )
   316→    return ctx, queue, queue.get("items", []), effective_cluster
   317→
   318→
   319→def _apply_queue_view_transforms(
   320→    *,
   321→    items: list[dict],
   322→    queue: dict,
   323→    opts: NextOptions,
   324→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   325→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   326→    collapse_plan_clusters: bool,
   327→) -> list[dict]:
   328→    visible = items
   329→    if collapse_plan_clusters and effective_cluster and plan_for_queue:
   330→        visible = filter_cluster_focus(visible, plan_for_queue, effective_cluster)
   331→    elif (
   332→        collapse_plan_clusters
   333→        and plan_for_queue
   334→        and not effective_cluster
   335→        and not plan_for_queue.get("active_cluster")
   336→    ):
   337→        visible = collapse_clusters(visible, plan_for_queue)
   338→
   339→    if opts.count:
   340→        visible = visible[: opts.count]
   341→        queue["items"] = visible
   342→        queue["total"] = len(visible)
   343→    return visible
   344→
   345→
   346→def _render_non_empty_queue(
   347→    *,
   348→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   349→    state: dict,
   350→    items: list[dict],
   351→    queue: dict,
   352→    plan_data: dict | None,
   353→    guardrail_warnings: list[str],
   354→    write_query_fn,
   355→    view: QueueViewConfig,
   356→    opts: NextOptions,
   357→    plan_for_queue: dict,
   358→    effective_cluster: str | None,
   359→    target_strict: float,
   360→    ctx,
   361→    resolve_lang_fn,
   362→) -> None:
   363→    lang = resolve_lang_fn(args)
   364→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   365→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   366→        state,
   367→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command=view.command_name, plan=plan_data),
   368→    )
   369→    payload = _write_next_payload(
   370→        queue=queue,
   371→        items=items,
   372→        state=state,
   373→        narrative=narrative,
   374→        plan_data=plan_data,
   375→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   376→        write_query_fn=write_query_fn,
   377→        command_name=view.command_name,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    if _emit_requested_output(opts, payload, items, command_name=view.command_name):
   381→        return
   382→
   383→    _render_terminal_queue_view(
   384→        queue=queue,
   385→        items=items,
   386→        state=state,
   387→        opts=opts,
   388→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   389→        plan_data=plan_data,
   390→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   391→        target_strict=target_strict,
   392→        ctx=ctx,
   393→        show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context,
   394→        show_execution_prompt=view.show_execution_prompt,
   395→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   396→    )
   397→
   398→
   399→def _build_and_render_queue_view(
   400→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   401→    state: dict,
   402→    config: dict,
   403→    *,
   404→    deps: QueueRenderDeps,
   405→    view: QueueViewConfig,
   406→) -> None:
   407→    """Build queue payload and render output for a queue surface."""
   408→    opts = NextOptions.from_args(args)
   409→    guardrail_warnings = triage_guardrail_messages(state=state)
   410→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   411→
   412→    plan = deps.load_plan_fn()
   413→    plan_for_queue = plan
   414→    plan_data = _plan_data_for_view(plan, show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context)
   415→    ctx, queue, items, effective_cluster = _resolve_queue_items(
   416→        state=state,
   417→        config=config,
   418→        opts=opts,
   419→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   420→        target_strict=target_strict,
   421→        build_work_queue_fn=deps.build_work_queue_fn,
   422→    )
   423→    items = _apply_queue_view_transforms(
   424→        items=items,
   425→        queue=queue,
   426→        opts=opts,
   427→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   428→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   429→        collapse_plan_clusters=view.collapse_plan_clusters,
   430→    )
   431→
   432→    if not items:
   433→        _render_empty_queue_view(
   434→            queue=queue,
   435→            items=items,
   436→            state=state,
   437→            plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   438→            plan_data=plan_data,
   439→            ctx=ctx,
   440→            target_strict=target_strict,
   441→            opts=opts,
   442→            guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   443→            write_query_fn=deps.write_query_fn,
   444→            command_name=view.command_name,
   445→            show_plan_context=view.show_plan_context,
   446→        )
   447→        return
   448→
   449→    _render_non_empty_queue(
   450→        args=args,
   451→        state=state,
   452→        items=items,
   453→        queue=queue,
   454→        plan_data=plan_data,
   455→        guardrail_warnings=guardrail_warnings,
   456→        write_query_fn=deps.write_query_fn,
   457→        view=view,
   458→        opts=opts,
   459→        plan_for_queue=plan_for_queue,
   460→        effective_cluster=effective_cluster,
   461→        target_strict=target_strict,
   462→        ctx=ctx,
   463→        resolve_lang_fn=deps.resolve_lang_fn,
   464→    )
   465→
   466→
   467→def build_and_render_execution_queue(
   468→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   469→    state: dict,
   470→    config: dict,
   471→    *,
   472→    deps: QueueRenderDeps | None = None,
   473→) -> None:
   474→    """Build queue payload and render output for `desloppify next`."""
   475→    build_and_render_queue(
   476→        args,
   477→        state,
   478→        config,
   479→        view=EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW,
   480→        deps=deps
   481→        or QueueRenderDeps(
   482→            build_work_queue_fn=build_execution_queue,
   483→        ),
   484→    )
   485→
   486→
   487→def build_and_render_backlog_queue(
   488→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   489→    state: dict,
   490→    config: dict,
   491→    *,
   492→    deps: QueueRenderDeps | None = None,
   493→) -> None:
   494→    """Build queue payload and render output for `desloppify backlog`."""
   495→    build_and_render_queue(
   496→        args,
   497→        state,
   498→        config,
   499→        view=BACKLOG_QUEUE_VIEW,
   500→        deps=deps
   501→        or QueueRenderDeps(
   502→            build_work_queue_fn=build_backlog_queue,
   503→        ),
   504→    )
   505→
   506→
   507→def build_and_render_queue(
   508→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   509→    state: dict,
   510→    config: dict,
   511→    *,
   512→    view: QueueViewConfig = EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW,
   513→    deps: QueueRenderDeps | None = None,
   514→) -> None:
   515→    """Build queue payload and render output using one stable view contract."""
   516→    _build_and_render_queue_view(
   517→        args,
   518→        state,
   519→        config,
   520→        deps=deps or QueueRenderDeps(build_work_queue_fn=build_execution_queue),
   521→        view=view,
   522→    )
   523→
   524→
   525→__all__ = [
   526→    "BACKLOG_QUEUE_VIEW",
   527→    "EXECUTION_QUEUE_VIEW",
   528→    "QueueRenderDeps",
   529→    "QueueViewConfig",
   530→    "build_and_render_backlog_queue",
   531→    "build_and_render_execution_queue",
   532→    "build_and_render_queue",
```

```
1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import replace
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    QueueVisibility,
    13→    WorkQueueResult,
    14→    _build_work_queue_with_visibility,
    15→    build_work_queue,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _subjective_threshold(state: StateModel, *, default: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE) -> float:
    20→    config = state.get("config", {})
    21→    raw_target = default
    22→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    23→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    24→    try:
    25→        value = float(raw_target)
    26→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    27→        value = default
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _queue_plan_from_options(options: QueueBuildOptions) -> dict | None:
    32→    if options.context is not None:
    33→        return options.context.plan
    34→    return options.plan
    35→
    36→
    37→def build_open_plan_queue(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    41→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    42→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    43→    opts = replace(options) if options is not None else QueueBuildOptions(count=None)
    44→    if options is None:
    45→        opts = replace(opts, status="open", include_subjective=True)
    46→    elif opts.status == QueueBuildOptions().status:
    47→        opts = replace(opts, status="open")
    48→    if opts.subjective_threshold == QueueBuildOptions().subjective_threshold:
    49→        opts = replace(opts, subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state))
    50→    return build_work_queue(
    51→        state,
    52→        options=opts,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def build_execution_queue(
    57→    state: StateModel,
    58→    *,
    59→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    60→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    61→    """Build the execution queue that follows the living plan when present."""
    62→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    63→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    64→        state,
    65→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    66→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    67→    )
    68→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    69→        state,
    70→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    71→        visibility=QueueVisibility.EXECUTION,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def build_backlog_queue(
    76→    state: StateModel,
    77→    *,
    78→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    79→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    80→    """Build the broader backlog, excluding work already tracked in the plan."""
    81→    options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    82→    ctx = options.context or queue_context(
    83→        state,
    84→        plan=_queue_plan_from_options(options),
    85→        target_strict=_subjective_threshold(state),
    86→    )
    87→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    88→        state,
    89→        options=replace(options, context=ctx),
    90→        visibility=QueueVisibility.BACKLOG,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "build_backlog_queue",
    96→    "build_execution_queue",
    97→    "build_open_plan_queue",
    98→]
    99→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue entrypoints for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.finalize import finalize_queue
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import resolve_queue_inputs
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import (
    10→    QueueBuildOptions,
    11→    QueueVisibility,
    12→    WorkQueueResult,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import group_queue_items
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.selection import select_queue_items
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→def build_work_queue(
    20→    state: StateModel,
    21→    *,
    22→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    23→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    24→    """Build the active canonical work queue for the current invocation."""
    25→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    26→        state,
    27→        options=options,
    28→        visibility=QueueVisibility.ALL,
    29→    )
    30→
    31→
    32→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    33→    state: StateModel,
    34→    *,
    35→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    36→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    39→
    40→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    41→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    42→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.
    43→    """
    44→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    45→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = resolve_queue_inputs(opts, state)
    46→    items = select_queue_items(
    47→        state,
    48→        opts=opts,
    49→        plan=plan,
    50→        scan_path=scan_path,
    51→        status=status,
    52→        threshold=threshold,
    53→        visibility=visibility,
    54→    )
    55→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)
    56→

    58→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
    59→    "QueueBuildOptions",
    60→    "QueueContext",

    63→    "_build_work_queue_with_visibility",
    64→    "build_work_queue",
    65→    "collapse_clusters",
```

```
1→"""Queue item selection from canonical snapshot or raw issue lists."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import gather_subjective_items
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, QueueVisibility
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import build_queue_snapshot
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→
    14→def select_queue_items(
    15→    state: StateModel,
    16→    *,
    17→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    18→    plan: dict | None,
    19→    scan_path: str | None,
    20→    status: str,
    21→    threshold: float,
    22→    visibility: str,
    23→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    24→    """Select the raw queue item list before ranking/finalization."""
    25→    if status == "open" and not opts.chronic:
    26→        snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=opts,
    29→            plan=plan,
    30→            target_strict=threshold,
    31→        )
    32→        return filter_snapshot_items(
    33→            items_for_visibility(snapshot=snapshot, visibility=visibility),
    34→            opts,
    35→        )
    36→
    37→    items = build_issue_items(
    38→        state,
    39→        scan_path=scan_path,
    40→        status_filter=status,
    41→        scope=opts.scope,
    42→        chronic=opts.chronic,
    43→    )
    44→    if opts.include_subjective and status == "all":
    45→        items += gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
    46→    return items
    47→
    48→
    49→def items_for_visibility(*, snapshot, visibility: str) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    50→    """Select the snapshot partition for one queue surface."""
    51→    if visibility == QueueVisibility.BACKLOG:
    52→        return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.backlog_items]
    53→    return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.execution_items]
    54→
    55→
    56→def filter_snapshot_items(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    59→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    60→    """Apply view-local filtering after snapshot partition selection."""
    61→    filtered = items
    62→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    63→        filtered = [
    64→            item for item in filtered
    65→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
    66→        ]
    67→    if opts.scope:
    68→        filtered = [
    69→            item for item in filtered
    70→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension" or scope_matches(item, opts.scope)
    71→        ]
    72→    return filtered
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = [
    76→    "filter_snapshot_items",
    77→    "items_for_visibility",
    78→    "select_queue_items",
    79→]
    80→
```

```
1→"""Queue ranking and plan-aware finalization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, WorkQueueResult
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
     7→    enrich_plan_metadata,
     8→    separate_skipped,
     9→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    10→    stamp_positions,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import new_item_ids as _new_item_ids
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    14→    enrich_with_impact,
    15→    group_queue_items,
    16→    item_explain,
    17→    item_sort_key,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    21→
    22→
    23→def finalize_queue(
    24→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    25→    *,
    26→    state: StateModel,
    27→    plan: dict | None,
    28→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    29→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    30→    """Apply ranking, plan ordering, limits, and explain metadata."""
    31→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    32→
    33→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
    34→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    35→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
    36→
    37→    total = len(items)
    38→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
    39→        items = items[:opts.count]
    40→    if opts.explain:
    41→        for item in items:
    42→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
    43→
    44→    return {
    45→        "items": items,
    46→        "total": total,
    47→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
    48→        "new_ids": new_ids,
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→def _plan_presort(
    53→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    54→    state: StateModel,
    55→    plan: dict | None,
    56→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    57→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting."""
    58→    if not plan:
    59→        return set(), []
    60→
    61→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
    62→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
    63→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
    64→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
    65→    items[:] = remaining
    66→    return new_ids, skipped
    67→
    68→
    69→def _plan_postsort(
    70→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    71→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
    72→    plan: dict | None,
    73→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions."""
    76→    if not plan:
    77→        return
    78→    if opts.include_skipped:
    79→        items.extend(skipped)
    80→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = ["finalize_queue"]
    84→
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
    10→    get_issue_description,
    11→    get_issue_note,
    12→    get_issue_override,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    15→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cluster_issue_ids(cluster: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    19→    """Return canonical cluster members after plan-load normalization."""
    20→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
    22→        return []
    23→    return [issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id]
    24→
    25→
    26→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    27→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    28→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    29→    if not scan_history:
    30→        return set()
    31→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    32→    if not threshold:
    33→        return set()
    34→    return {
    35→        issue_id
    36→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    37→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    42→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    43→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    44→
    45→    for item in items:
    46→        issue_id = item["id"]
    47→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    48→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    49→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    50→        if description:
    51→            item["plan_description"] = description
    52→        if note:
    53→            item["plan_note"] = note
    54→        if override.get("cluster"):
    55→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    56→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    57→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    58→                "name": cluster_name,
    59→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    60→                "total_items": len(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data)),
    61→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    62→            }
    63→
    64→
    65→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    66→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    67→    plan: dict,
    68→    new_ids: set[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    71→
    72→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    73→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    74→    """
    75→    queue_order = list(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    76→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order)
    77→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    78→
    79→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    80→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    81→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    82→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    83→
    84→    for item in items:
    85→        item_id = item["id"]
    86→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    87→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    88→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    89→
    90→
    91→def separate_skipped(
    92→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    93→    plan: dict,
    94→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    95→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    96→
    97→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    98→    """
    99→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return items, []
   102→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   103→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   104→    for item in items:
   105→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   106→            skipped.append(item)
   107→        else:
   108→            non_skipped.append(item)
   109→    return non_skipped, skipped
   110→
   111→
   112→def filter_cluster_focus(
   113→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   114→    plan: dict,
   115→    cluster: str | None,
   116→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   117→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   118→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   119→    if not effective_cluster:
   120→        return items
   121→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   122→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   123→    cluster_member_ids = set(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data))
   124→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   125→        return items
   126→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   127→
   128→
   129→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   131→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   132→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   133→
   134→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   135→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   136→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   137→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   138→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   139→            if skip_entry:
   140→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   141→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   142→                if skip_reason:
   143→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   144→
   145→
   146→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   147→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   148→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   149→    if meta:
   150→        return meta.action_type
   151→    return "manual_fix"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _build_cluster_meta(
   155→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   156→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   157→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   158→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   159→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   160→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   161→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   162→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   163→        action_type = "reorganize"
   164→    else:
   165→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   166→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   167→            action_type = "refactor"
   168→
   169→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   170→    total_in_cluster = len(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster_data))
   171→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   172→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   173→    else:
   174→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   175→
   176→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   177→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   178→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   179→        autofix_hint = action
   180→    else:
   181→        primary_command = action or f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   182→        autofix_hint = None
   183→
   184→    estimated_impact = max(
   185→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   186→    )
   187→
   188→    return {
   189→        "id": cluster_name,
   190→        "kind": "cluster",
   191→        "action_type": action_type,
   192→        "summary": summary,
   193→        "members": members,
   194→        "member_count": len(members),
   195→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   196→        "autofix_hint": autofix_hint,
   197→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   198→        "cluster_auto": bool(cluster_data.get("auto")),
   199→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   200→        "confidence": "high",
   201→        "detector": detector,
   202→        "file": "",
   203→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   204→        "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
   205→    }
   206→
   207→
   208→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   209→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   210→
   211→    Both auto-clusters and manual (triage) clusters are collapsed.  Manual
   212→    clusters are inserted at the front in plan order so triage-prioritised
   213→    work appears before auto-clustered mechanical items.
   214→    """
   215→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   216→    if not clusters:
   217→        return items
   218→
   219→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   220→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   221→        for issue_id in _cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   222→            # Manual clusters take priority when an issue is in both
   223→            if issue_id not in fid_to_cluster or not cluster.get("auto"):
   224→                fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   225→
   226→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   227→        return items
   228→
   229→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   230→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   231→    for item in items:
   232→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   233→        if cname:
   234→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   235→
   236→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   237→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   238→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   239→        if len(members) < 2:
   240→            continue
   241→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   242→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   243→        )
   244→
   245→    # Collect manual cluster names in plan order (for front-insertion)
   246→    manual_names = [
   247→        name for name in clusters
   248→        if not clusters[name].get("auto") and name in meta_items
   249→    ]
   250→
   251→    # Walk in order: replace first auto-cluster member with meta-item,
   252→    # skip subsequent members.  Manual cluster members are always skipped
   253→    # (they'll be inserted at the front).
   254→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   255→    rest: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   256→    for item in items:
   257→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   258→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   259→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   260→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   261→                # Auto-clusters collapse in-place; manual clusters go to front
   262→                if clusters.get(cname, {}).get("auto"):
   263→                    rest.append(meta_items[cname])
   264→            # skip individual member
   265→        else:
   266→            rest.append(item)
   267→
   268→    # Manual clusters at the front in plan order, then everything else
   269→    manual_result = [meta_items[name] for name in manual_names if name in seen_clusters]
   270→    return manual_result + rest
   271→
   272→
   273→__all__ = [
   274→    "collapse_clusters",
   275→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   276→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   277→    "new_item_ids",
   278→    "separate_skipped",
   279→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   280→    "stamp_positions",
   281→]
   282→
```

```
1→"""Workflow-specific synthetic work queue item builders."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import shlex
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     9→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    10→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    11→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    12→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    13→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    14→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import (
    17→    postflight_scan_pending,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.workflow import (
    20→    pending_import_scores_meta,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    23→    triage_manual_stage_command,
    24→    triage_run_stages_command,
    25→    triage_runner_commands,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkflowActionItem
    28→
    29→
    30→def _workflow_action_item(
    31→    *,
    32→    item_id: str,
    33→    summary: str,
    34→    primary_command: str,
    35→    detail: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    36→) -> WorkflowActionItem:
    37→    return {
    38→        "id": item_id,
    39→        "tier": 1,
    40→        "confidence": "high",
    41→        "detector": "workflow",
    42→        "file": ".",
    43→        "kind": "workflow_action",
    44→        "summary": summary,
    45→        "detail": detail or {},
    46→        "primary_command": primary_command,
    47→        "blocked_by": [],
    48→        "is_blocked": False,
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→def _confirm_attestation_hint(stage: str) -> str:
    53→    if stage == "observe":
    54→        return "I have thoroughly reviewed..."
    55→    if stage == "reflect":
    56→        return "My strategy accounts for..."
    57→    if stage == "organize":
    58→        return "This plan is correct..."
    59→    if stage == "enrich":
    60→        return "Steps are executor-ready..."
    61→    if stage == "sense-check":
    62→        return "Content and structure verified..."
    63→    return "..."
    64→
    65→
    66→def _runner_planning_tools(
    67→    *,
    68→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    69→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    70→) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    71→    tools = [
    72→        {"label": label, "command": command}
    73→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    74→    ]
    75→    if manual_fallback:
    76→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    77→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    78→    return tools
    79→
    80→
    81→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    82→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    84→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    85→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    86→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    87→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    88→            return stage, True
    89→
    90→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    91→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    92→        if sid not in order:
    93→            continue
    94→        return stage, False
    95→
    96→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    97→        return "commit", False
    98→
    99→    return None, False
   100→
   101→
   102→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
   103→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   104→    if stage is None:
   105→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   106→    if needs_confirm:
   107→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
   108→        return (
   109→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
   110→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
   111→        )
   112→    if stage == "commit":
   113→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   114→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   118→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   119→    if needs_confirm:
   120→        return []
   121→    if stage == "commit":
   122→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   123→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   124→        )
   125→    if stage:
   126→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   127→            only_stages=stage,
   128→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   129→        )
   130→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   131→        only_stages="observe",
   132→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   133→    )
   134→
   135→
   136→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   137→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
   138→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   142→
   143→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   144→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   145→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   146→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   147→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   148→
   149→    return _workflow_action_item(
   150→        item_id=WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   151→        summary=f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   152→        detail={
   153→            "strict": strict,
   154→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   155→            "delta": delta,
   156→            "planning_tools": _runner_planning_tools(
   157→                only_stages="observe",
   158→                manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   159→            ),
   160→        },
   161→        primary_command=triage_run_stages_command(only_stages="observe"),
   162→    )
   163→
   164→
   165→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   166→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if queued."""
   167→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    return _workflow_action_item(
   171→        item_id=WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   172→        summary="Triage imported review findings into an execution plan before more code changes",
   173→        detail={
   174→            "planning_tools": _create_plan_planning_tools(plan),
   175→        },
   176→        primary_command=_create_plan_primary_command(plan),
   177→    )
   178→
   179→
   180→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   181→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::import-scores`` if queued."""
   182→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   183→        return None
   184→    meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   185→    import_file = (meta.import_file if meta is not None else "").strip() or "issues.json"
   186→    quoted_import_file = shlex.quote(import_file)
   187→    packet_sha = meta.packet_sha256 if meta is not None else ""
   188→    explanation = (
   189→        "Review issues were imported but assessment scores were skipped "
   190→        "(untrusted source). Re-import the same batch with attestation to update dimension scores."
   191→    )
   192→    if import_file != "issues.json":
   193→        explanation += f" Expected import file: `{import_file}`."
   194→    if packet_sha:
   195→        explanation += f" Expected packet sha256: `{packet_sha}`."
   196→
   197→    return _workflow_action_item(
   198→        item_id=WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   199→        summary="Import assessment scores with attestation",
   200→        detail={
   201→            "explanation": explanation,
   202→            "expected_import_file": import_file,
   203→            "packet_sha256": packet_sha,
   204→        },
   205→        primary_command=(
   206→            f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import_file} --attested-external "
   207→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   208→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   209→        ),
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   214→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if queued."""
   215→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   216→        return None
   217→
   218→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   219→
   220→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   221→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   222→
   223→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta
   224→    # so the user sees old-baseline → current, not current → current.
   225→    prev_start = (plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   226→    if prev_start is None:
   227→        prev_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   228→    delta = round(strict - prev_start, 1) if prev_start is not None else None
   229→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   230→
   231→    return _workflow_action_item(
   232→        item_id=WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   233→        summary=f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   234→        detail={
   235→            "strict": strict,
   236→            "previous_plan_start_strict": prev_start,
   237→            "delta": delta,
   238→        },
   239→        primary_command=(
   240→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   241→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   242→        ),
   243→    )
   244→
   245→
   246→def build_run_scan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   247→    """Build a synthetic item for the first post-flight scan step."""
   248→    if not postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   249→        return None
   250→
   251→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   252→        item_id=WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
   253→        summary="Run post-flight scan to refresh queue and surface follow-up review work.",
   254→        detail={
   255→            "phase": "postflight_scan",
   256→        },
   257→        primary_command="desloppify scan",
   258→    )
   259→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"
   260→    return item
   261→
   262→
   263→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   265→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   266→        return []
   267→    deferred: list[str] = []
   268→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   269→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   270→            continue
   271→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   272→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   273→    deferred.sort()
   274→    return deferred
   275→
   276→
   277→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   278→    plan: dict,
   279→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   280→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   281→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   282→    if not deferred_set:
   283→        return 0, 0
   284→
   285→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   286→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   287→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   288→
   289→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   290→    cluster_count = 0
   291→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   292→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   293→            continue
   294→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   295→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   296→            continue
   297→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   298→        if not matched:
   299→            continue
   300→        cluster_count += 1
```

```
   307→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
```
