```
1→"""Canonical queue snapshot for phase and visibility decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    11→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    12→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    16→    planned_objective_ids as _planned_objective_ids,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.snapshot import build_triage_snapshot
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
    22→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    23→    build_subjective_items,
    24→    build_triage_stage_items,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    27→    build_communicate_score_item,
    28→    build_create_plan_item,
    29→    build_deferred_disposition_item,
    30→    build_import_scores_item,
    31→    build_run_scan_item,
    32→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    33→)
    34→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    35→
    36→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    37→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    38→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    39→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    40→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    41→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueSnapshot:
    46→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    47→
    48→    phase: str
    49→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    52→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    53→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    54→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    55→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    56→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    57→    objective_in_scope_count: int
    58→    planned_objective_count: int
    59→    objective_execution_count: int
    60→    objective_backlog_count: int
    61→    subjective_initial_count: int
    62→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    63→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    64→    triage_pending_count: int
    65→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    66→
    67→
    68→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    69→    if options is None:
    70→        return default
    71→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    75→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    76→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    77→        return state.get("scan_path")
    78→    return scan_path
    79→
    80→
    81→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    82→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    83→        return True
    84→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    85→    if not scores:
    86→        return True
    87→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    91→    return (
    92→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
    93→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    94→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    99→    return [
   100→        item for item in items
   101→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   106→    plan: dict | None,
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   109→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   111→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   112→        return review_issue_items
   113→
   114→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   115→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   116→        return []
   117→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   118→        return []
   119→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   120→        return []
   121→    return review_issue_items
   122→
   123→
   124→def _subjective_partitions(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   128→    threshold: float,
   129→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   130→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   131→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   132→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   133→    return initial, postflight
   134→
   135→
   136→def _workflow_partitions(
   137→    plan: dict | None,
   138→    state: StateModel,
   139→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   140→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   141→        return [], [], []
   142→    scan_items = [
   143→        item
   144→        for item in (
   145→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   146→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   147→        )
   148→        if item is not None
   149→    ]
   150→    postflight_workflow = [
   151→        item
   152→        for item in (
   153→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   154→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   155→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   156→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   157→        )
   158→        if item is not None
   159→    ]
   160→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   161→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   162→
   163→
   164→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   165→    *,
   166→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   167→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   168→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   169→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   170→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   171→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   172→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   173→    triage_blocks_execution: bool,
   174→) -> str:
   175→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   176→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   177→    if objective_items and not triage_blocks_execution:
   178→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   179→    if scan_items:
   180→        return PHASE_SCAN
   181→    if triage_blocks_execution and postflight_workflow_items:
   182→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   183→    if triage_blocks_execution and triage_items:
   184→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   185→    if objective_items:
   186→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   187→    if postflight_review_items:
   188→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   189→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   190→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   191→    if triage_items:
   192→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   193→    return PHASE_SCAN
   194→
   195→
   196→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   197→    phase: str,
   198→    *,
   199→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   200→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   201→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   202→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   203→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   204→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   205→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   206→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   207→        return initial_review_items
   208→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   209→        return objective_items
   210→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   211→        deferred_items = [
   212→            item for item in scan_items
   213→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   214→        ]
   215→        if deferred_items:
   216→            return deferred_items
   217→        return [
   218→            item for item in scan_items
   219→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   220→        ]
   221→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   222→        return postflight_review_items
   223→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   224→        return postflight_workflow_items
   225→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   226→        return triage_items
   227→    return []
   228→
   229→
   230→def build_queue_snapshot(
   231→    state: StateModel,
   232→    *,
   233→    options: object | None = None,
   234→    plan: dict | None = None,
   235→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   236→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   237→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   238→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   239→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   240→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   241→    )
   242→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   243→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   244→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   245→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   246→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   247→
   248→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   249→        state,
   250→        scan_path=scan_path,
   251→        status_filter="open",
   252→        scope=scope,
   253→        chronic=chronic,
   254→    )
   255→    objective_items = [
   256→        item for item in all_issue_items
   257→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   258→    ]
   259→    planned_ids = _planned_objective_ids(
   260→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   261→        effective_plan,
   262→    )
   263→    planned_objective_items = [
   264→        item for item in objective_items
   265→        if item.get("id", "") in planned_ids
   266→    ]
   267→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   268→    triage_snapshot = (
   269→        build_triage_snapshot(effective_plan, state)
   270→        if isinstance(effective_plan, dict)
   271→        else None
   272→    )
   273→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   274→        effective_plan,
   275→        state,
   276→        review_issue_items,
   277→    )
   278→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   279→        state,
   280→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   281→        threshold=target_strict,
   282→    )
   283→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *executable_review_items]
   284→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   285→        effective_plan,
   286→        state,
   287→    )
   288→
   289→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   290→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   291→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   292→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   293→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   294→        scan_items=scan_items,
   295→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   296→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   297→        triage_items=triage_items,
   298→        triage_blocks_execution=bool(
   299→            review_issue_items
   300→            and triage_snapshot is not None
   301→            and triage_snapshot.requires_plan_before_execution
   302→        ),
   303→    )
   304→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   305→        phase,
   306→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   307→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   308→        scan_items=scan_items,
   309→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   310→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   311→        triage_items=triage_items,
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   315→    backlog_items = [
   316→        item for item in (
   317→            [
   318→                *planned_objective_items,
   319→                *initial_review_items,
   320→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   321→                *review_issue_items,
   322→                *scan_items,
   323→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   324→                *triage_items,
   325→            ]
   326→        )
   327→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   328→    ]
   329→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   330→        1 for item in planned_objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   331→    )
   332→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(planned_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   333→
   334→    return QueueSnapshot(
   335→        phase=phase,
   336→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   337→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   338→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   339→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   340→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   341→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   342→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   343→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   344→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   345→        planned_objective_count=len(planned_objective_items),
   346→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   347→            1
   348→            for item in execution_items
   349→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   350→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
```
```

1→"""Synthetic work-queue item builders and dimension scoring.
     2→
     3→Builds workflow stage items, score checkpoint items, create-plan items,
     4→subjective dimension items, and subjective score lookups.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from typing import Any
    10→

    28→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,

    30→)
    31→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    32→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    33→    TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
    34→    TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
    35→    triage_manual_stage_command,
    36→    triage_run_stages_command,
    37→    triage_runner_commands,

    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Dimension key normalization
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    51→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    52→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    53→    target = cleaned.lower()
    54→
    55→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    56→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    57→            return str(dim_key)
    58→    return slugify(cleaned)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    63→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    64→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    65→    return {
    66→        cleaned.lower(),
    67→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    68→        slugify(cleaned),
    69→        canonical.lower(),
    70→        slugify(canonical),
    71→    }
    72→
    73→
    74→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    75→# Subjective strict scores
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→
    78→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    79→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    80→    if not dim_scores:
    81→        return {}
    82→
    83→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    84→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    85→    for entry in entries:
    86→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    87→        if not name:
    88→            continue
    89→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    90→        [REDACTED](name)
    91→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    92→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    93→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    94→            if not key:
    95→                continue
    96→            aliases.add(key)
    97→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    98→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    99→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   100→        for alias in aliases:
   101→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   102→    return scores
   103→
   104→
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→# Synthetic item builders
   107→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108→
   109→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   111→
   112→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   113→    """
   114→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→    order_set = set(order)
   116→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   117→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   118→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   119→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    present_names = {
   121→        name
   122→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   123→        if sid in present_ids
   124→    }
   125→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   126→    if not present_names:
   127→        return []
   128→
   129→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   130→    open_review_count = sum(
   131→        1 for f in issues.values()
   132→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   133→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   134→    )
   135→
   136→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   137→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   138→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   139→        if name not in present_names:
   140→            continue
   141→        if name in confirmed:
   142→            continue
   143→
   144→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   145→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   146→        blocked_by = sorted(
   147→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   148→        )
   149→
   150→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   151→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   152→
   153→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   154→            "id": sid,
   155→            "tier": 1,
   156→            "confidence": "high",
   157→            "detector": "triage",
   158→            "file": ".",
   159→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   160→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   161→            "detail": {
   162→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   163→                "stage": name,
   164→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   165→                "runner_commands": [
   166→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   167→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   168→                ],
   169→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   170→            },
   171→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   172→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   173→        }
   174→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   175→        items.append(item)
   176→    return items
   177→
   178→
   179→def build_subjective_items(
   180→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   181→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   182→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   183→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   184→    if not dim_scores:
   185→        return []
   186→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   187→
   188→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   189→    if not subjective_entries:
   190→        return []
   191→    unassessed_dims = {
   192→        str(name).strip()
   193→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   194→            dim_scores
   195→        )
   196→    }
   197→
   198→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   199→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   200→    for issue in issues.values():
   201→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   202→            continue
   203→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   204→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   205→            if dim_key:
   206→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   207→
   208→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   209→    def _prepare_command(
   210→        cli_keys: list[str],
   211→        *,
   212→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   213→    ) -> str:
   214→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   215→        if cli_keys:
   216→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   217→        if force_review_rerun:
   218→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   219→        return command
   220→
   221→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   222→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   223→        if not name:
   224→            continue
   225→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   226→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   227→            continue
   228→
   229→        [REDACTED](name)
   230→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   231→        cli_keys = [
   232→            str(key).strip().lower()
   233→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   234→            if str(key).strip()
   235→        ]
   236→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   237→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   238→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   239→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   240→            name in unassessed_dims
   241→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   242→        )
   243→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   244→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   245→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   246→        if open_review > 0:
   247→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   248→        else:
   249→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   250→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   251→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   252→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   253→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   254→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   255→            "file": ".",
   256→            "confidence": "medium",
   257→            "summary": summary,
   258→            "detail": {
   259→                "dimension_name": name,
   260→                "dimension": dim_key,
   261→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   262→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   263→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   264→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   265→            },
   266→            "status": "open",
   267→        }
   268→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   269→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   270→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   271→        items.append(item)
   272→    return items
   273→
   274→
   275→__all__ = [
   276→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   277→    "build_create_plan_item",
   278→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   279→    "build_import_scores_item",
   280→    "build_run_scan_item",
   281→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   282→    "build_subjective_items",
   283→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   284→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   285→]
   286→
```

```
def item_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.

    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.

    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
    """
    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")

    if plan_pos is not None:
        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
        if kind == "workflow_stage":
            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
            stage_idx = _workflow_stage_index(item)
            return (_TIER_PLANNED, plan_pos, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
        return (_TIER_PLANNED, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))

    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
    group = _TIER_NEW if is_new else _TIER_EXISTING
    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))


__all__ = [
    "build_issue_items",
    "enrich_with_impact",
    "item_explain",
    "item_sort_key",
    "subjective_score_value",
    "group_queue_items",
]
```

```
   348→def test_unplanned_objective_items_dont_block_postflight():
   349→    """Unplanned objective items don't keep the system in EXECUTE phase.
   350→
   351→    When all planned work drains, unplanned objective items should not block
   352→    phase transitions to postflight work. The has_unplanned_objective_blockers
   353→    flag is still set for informational purposes.
   354→
   355→    The split only applies when the plan has at least one tracked objective
   356→    (i.e. post-triage). Pre-triage plans treat all objectives as planned.
   357→    """
   358→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   359→
   360→    state = _state(
   361→        [
   362→            _issue("smells::src/a.py::planned", detector="smells"),
   363→            _issue("smells::src/b.py::unplanned", detector="smells"),
   364→        ],
   365→        dimension_scores={
   366→            "Naming quality": {
   367→                "score": 70.0,
   368→                "strict": 70.0,
   369→                "failing": 1,
   370→                "detectors": {
   371→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   372→                },
   373→            },
   374→        },
   375→    )
   376→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   377→        "naming_quality": {
   378→            "score": 70.0,
   379→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   380→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   381→        }
   382→    }
   383→
   384→    # Plan has one tracked objective — simulates post-triage state
   385→    plan = empty_plan()
   386→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"strict": 75.0}
   387→    plan["queue_order"] = ["smells::src/a.py::planned"]
   388→
   389→    queue = build_execution_queue(
   390→        state,
   391→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   392→            count=None,
   393→            include_subjective=True,
   394→            plan=plan,
   395→        ),
   396→    )
   397→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   398→    # Only planned item appears — unplanned item does NOT block
   399→    assert ids == ["smells::src/a.py::planned"]
   400→    assert "smells::src/b.py::unplanned" not in ids

   406→def test_skipped_objective_items_dont_block_subjective():
   407→    """Plan-skipped objective items are removed before lifecycle filter,
   408→    so they don't block subjective reassessment items.
   409→    """
   410→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   411→
   412→    objective_issues = [
   413→        _issue(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   414→        for c in "abcd"
   415→    ]
   416→    state = _state(
   417→        objective_issues,
   418→        dimension_scores={
   419→            "Naming quality": {
   420→                "score": 70.0,
   421→                "strict": 70.0,
   422→                "failing": 1,
   423→                "detectors": {
   424→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   425→                },
   426→            },
   427→        },
   428→    )
   429→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   430→        "naming_quality": {
   431→            "score": 70.0,
   432→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   433→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   434→        }
   435→    }
   436→
   437→    # Skip ALL objective issues in the plan
   438→    plan = empty_plan()
   439→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   440→        "subjective::naming_quality",
   441→        "smells::src/a.py::x",
   442→        "smells::src/b.py::x",
   443→        "smells::src/c.py::x",
   444→        "smells::src/d.py::x",
   445→    ]
   446→    plan["skipped"] = {
   447→        "smells::src/a.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   448→        "smells::src/b.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   449→        "smells::src/c.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   450→        "smells::src/d.py::x": {"reason": "deferred"},
   451→    }
   452→
   453→    queue = build_work_queue(
   454→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, plan=plan,
   455→    )
   456→    ids = [i["id"] for i in queue["items"]]
   457→    # All objective items skipped → lifecycle filter sees no objective work
   458→    # → but deferred disposition precedes subjective in lifecycle
   459→    assert "workflow::deferred-disposition" in ids
   460→    # Subjective items are gated behind deferred disposition
   461→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   462→    assert len(subj_ids) == 0
```

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a final summary document by gathering the key facts:

Based on my thorough exploration of the Reigh repository's desloppify state files and the desloppify source code, here is my comprehensive finding report:

## Queue Ordering Behavior: Scan Appears After Triage Items

### Evidence from Reigh State Files

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh/.desloppify/`:

1. **query.json** (latest `desloppify next` output):
   - Shows `"workflow::run-scan"` as the first and only item in the execution queue
   - Queue position: 1
   - Summary: "Run post-flight scan to refresh queue and surface follow-up review work."

2. **plan.json** (living plan structure):
   - Contains `queue_order` array with 40+ entries
   - First two items: `"workflow::communicate-score"`, `"workflow::create-plan"`
   - Items 3-38: review issues (`review::.::holistic::*` pattern)
   - Items 39-40: `"triage::sense-check"`, `"triage::commit"`
   - **`workflow::run-scan` is NOT in the queue_order array**

3. **Triage run logs** (20260312_214829):
   - Successfully completed organize stage
   - Plan creation commands all reference triage stages and review issues
   - No explicit reference to `run-scan` ordering in the organize output

### Architecture: How the Queue is Built

The issue stems from the **queue snapshot and lifecycle phase logic** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/`:

#### Key Code Flow (snapshot.py):

1. **_workflow_partitions()** (lines 136-161) builds synthetic items:
   - Calls `build_run_scan_item(plan)` which returns an item if `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` is true
   - Returns `(scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items)`
   - scan_items = [deferred_disposition, run_scan] (if applicable)
   - postflight_workflow_items = [score_checkpoint, import_scores, create_plan, communicate_score]
   - triage_items = triage stages from `build_triage_stage_items()`

2. **_phase_for_snapshot()** (lines 164-193) determines execution phase:
   - If `PHASE_SCAN` is active, it means: no objective items left AND scan items exist
   - Returns `PHASE_SCAN` (line 179-180) when `scan_items` has items and no triage blocks execution

3. **_execution_items_for_phase()** (lines 196-227) selects what executes:
   - When phase == PHASE_SCAN: returns only scan_items (lines 210-220)
   - When phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT: returns triage_items

#### Key Code Flow (finalize.py):

1. **finalize_queue()** (lines 23-49):
   - Calls `_plan_presort()` which stamps `_plan_position` via `stamp_plan_sort_keys()`
   - Sorts items using `item_sort_key()` 
   - Calls `_plan_postsort()` to stamp queue positions

2. **item_sort_key()** (ranking.py):
   - Items WITH `_plan_position` (in queue_order): sort by plan position first
   - Items WITHOUT `_plan_position` (not in queue_order): sort by natural ranking
   - Natural ranking for workflow items: sorts before triage stages

### The Core Problem

**Synthetic workflow items like `workflow::run-scan` and `triage::*` stages are NOT added to `plan.json`'s `queue_order` array.** Instead:

1. They are generated dynamically at queue build time in `snapshot.py`
2. They get `_plan_position = None` in `stamp_plan_sort_keys()` (line 87)
3. They fall into the "natural ranking" tier in `item_sort_key()`
4. Natural ranking sorts workflow actions BEFORE triage stages (test confirms this: test_triage_stages_sort_after_workflow_in_natural_ranking)

### Why This Manifests in Reigh

The Reigh project shows:
- **Plan phase**: `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT` or `PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT` (because objective work is drained and triage stages are in queue_order)
- **Triage items in queue_order** (lines 44-45 of plan.json): `"triage::sense-check"`, `"triage::commit"`
- **But scan item NOT in queue_order**: So `workflow::run-scan` gets natural ranking
- **Execution visibility logic**: The `build_run_scan_item()` sets `item["execution_visibility"] = "always"` (synthetic_workflow.py line 259), which forces it into execution queue
- **Result**: Scan item appears with natural ranking (higher priority than triage items without explicit plan position)

### Root Cause

The inconsistency is that:
- Review issues and triage stages are explicitly added to `queue_order` by the triage process
- Synthetic workflow items are injected dynamically and never get plan positions
- This causes them to sort by natural ranking rules rather than explicit plan intent

### File Paths

Key implementation files:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py` (lines 136-227) — phase determination and item selection
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` (lines 246-260) — run-scan item builder
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py` (lines 65-89) — plan position stamping
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` — item sort key logic
